# CHAPTER 14 — GRAVITATION: DEFECT GEOMETRY, THE SLAVED CONE, AND THE EQUILIBRIUM VACUUM

**Learning goals.** (1) Understand the Kondo–Kröner–Kleinert dictionary: defect densities ARE torsion and curvature, at theorem grade. (2) Derive the Einstein–Cartan structure and its observational signature profile (no extra GW polarizations; contact-only torsion; the bounce). (3) Understand Sakharov induction, the one-line equivalence principle, and the cone-slaving theorem with the UHECR pincer — the program's showcase of passing a lethal bound *by theorem*. (4) Evade Weinberg–Witten legally, by premises. (5) Understand the Gibbs–Duhem vacuum-energy cancellation, the α > 0 sign theorem, and the growth-correlated w(z) — including the pre-bound escape hatch. (6) Meet the Machian-dragged foliation.

## 14.1 The KBKK dictionary: defects are geometry

The import that anchors the sector, and it is a *theorem*, not an analogy [IM: Kondo 1952; Bilby 1955; Kröner 1958–80s; Kleinert's *Gauge Fields in Condensed Matter*]: in a solid with a continuous distribution of defects, the natural differential geometry of the material manifold is Riemann–Cartan, and the dictionary is exact —

**dislocation density = torsion T^λ_{μν}; disclination density = curvature R^λ_{σμν}.** (14.1)

Why this is true, at teaching depth: parallel-transport a lattice vector around a small loop in a defected crystal. If the loop encloses dislocations, the transported vector fails to close the loop by the *Burgers vector* — translation holonomy — which is precisely what torsion measures (the failure of infinitesimal parallelograms to close). If the loop encloses disclinations, the vector returns *rotated* by the Frank angle — rotation holonomy — which is precisely curvature. A crystal engineer measuring Burgers and Frank densities *is doing* Riemann–Cartan geometry with calipers. GUM inherits the dictionary wholesale: coarse-grained defect distributions of the substrate define (g_μν, T^λ_μν); matter knots — which carry the Cosserat spin density conjugate to the asymmetric stress (Chapter 1's torque channel!) — source torsion; energy–momentum sources curvature. The stiff-medium field equations are **Einstein–Cartan**: general relativity's closest cousin, in which torsion is *algebraically* locked to spin density (no derivatives of torsion in the action ⟹ torsion does not propagate ⟹ it vanishes identically in matter-free regions and GR is recovered exactly in vacuum).

**The signature profile, audited against data.** (i) **No extra gravitational-wave polarizations** — torsion doesn't propagate, so GUM's GW sector is GR's two tensor modes, consistent with LIGO/Virgo polarization analyses; note how nontrivial a pass this is: most modified-gravity frameworks *fail exactly here*. (ii) Spin–torsion contact corrections scale as (spin density)² and need ∼10⁵⁴ kg/m³ to matter — irrelevant in neutron stars by ~36 orders; the sector's arena is the very early universe, where (iii) the repulsive spin–spin contact term generically replaces the singularity with a **bounce** [DW] — a remote but genuine signature class (suppressed/oscillatory primordial features vs the inflationary baseline).

## 14.2 Sakharov induction and the equivalence principle in one line

Where is Newton's constant? Not in the action — GUM's action (Chapter 3) has no Einstein–Hilbert term. It is *induced* [IM: Sakharov 1967]: integrating the substrate's modes to one loop on a slowly-varying defect-geometric background generates

Γ_ind = Σ_s (N_s/32π²) ∫√(−g_s) [2Λ_s⁴𝔞₀ + Λ_s²𝔞₁R[g_s] + 𝔞₂ ln Λ_s·(R²-terms)], (14.2)

sector-summed over B2±, B3, B4, and the knot band-edges, each mode species propagating on its own effective metric g_s = ḡ + 2δ_sc²n⊗n (the common acoustic metric ḡ deformed by that sector's fractional cone offset δ_s). The Λ²𝔞₁R term *is* the Einstein–Hilbert action, with **G ∼ c³/(ħN_effΛ_UV²), Λ_UV ∼ 1/a: gravity is feeble because the lattice is stiff and fine** ⟦rev [F-R14, E.6/VI.C]⟧ — *with this edition's flag: the sign of the induced G depends on the sector-summed 𝔞₁ weights, and the external audit found the B2 weight negative on the corpus's own action (see §14.3 revised); a positive N_eff here is currently conditional on the P-acoustic repair* ⟦/rev⟧ — the hierarchy problem's GUM answer is a stiffness ratio, the same ladder §13.5 gestured at for α. (The Λ⁴ term — the vacuum-energy bomb — is quarantined to §14.4; do not defuse it prematurely.) **The equivalence principle, one line** [DF]: every clock, rod, and particle is an excitation of the *same* medium reading the *same* acoustic metric ḡ — universality of free fall by construction, not by tuning. MICROSCOPE's η = (−1.5 ± 2.7)×10⁻¹⁵ is compliance data, and — mark the logical form — **any confirmed EP violation, at any level, kills the sector outright**: GUM is *more* falsifiable here than frameworks that could absorb a small violation, and the course's standing instruction is to treat maximal killability as a feature you hunt for in your own work.

## 14.3 The cone-slaving theorem and the UHECR pincer: how GW170817 was passed by theorem

**The exposure, stated as the red team stated it.** GW170817/GRB 170817A bounds |c_GW − c_γ|/c ≲ 10⁻¹⁵. GUM's graviton is induced — a collective mode of the substrate's fluctuations. If its cone differed from the photon's at 10⁻¹⁴, *the program died in August 2017 and didn't know it.* For months of the corpus's history this was flag R-M3, ranked the single most dangerous open item: an already-taken measurement that might already be a corpse.

⟦rev [F-R14, h33 Part B — theory-audit/h34_positivity.md; paper VI.C/E.6 as revised]⟧ **Theorem 14.1 (cone slaving)** [regraded: OPEN-DEFECT, conditional on P-acoustic — was DF]. Expand each sector's √(−g_s)R[g_s] in δ_s and collect the transverse-traceless kinetic operator. The original proof continued: "because each heat-kernel weight w_s = N_sΛ_s²𝔞₁^{(s)} is *positive*…" — and there is the asserted-not-derived sub-lemma. The external audit did the derivation: on the corpus's own action, the B2 sector has p = q − 1, which forces **𝔞₁^(B2) < 0 in every reading** — the positivity premise *fails*. The consequences fork into a printed disjunction: either the negative weight inverts the induced G (fatal on its face) or the theory exits the convex hull the averaging theorem needs (the weighted "mean" is no longer a mean, and the slaving bound loses its automatic status). The corpus's candidate rescue is **P-acoustic** — a new postulate tying the B2 weight to the common acoustic metric — with its costs priced in the v3.0-ext paper (VI.1), not waved through; and the knot supertrace carries its own erratum in the same entry. Until P-acoustic is discharged, the theorem's conclusion holds as a *conditional*: IF the repaired weights are admissible, the induced graviton propagates on the **loop-weighted mean of the sector cones**: ⟦/rev⟧

**c_GW² = Σ_s w_s c_s² / Σ_s w_s ⟹ |Δc_GW/c| ≤ max_s |δ_s(E ∼ Λ_UV)| ≡ δ_UV.** (14.3)

A convex combination cannot exceed its extremes: **the graviton cone is not an independent constitutive fact — it is slaved to the inter-sector splitting**, and the question "is c_GW = c?" reduces to "how split are the matter and light cones at the cutoff?" ∎

**The pincer** [DW]. Now the beautiful accident of scales. The loop weights peak at the cutoff — where Chapter 5's C1 flow makes δ *largest* (δ runs down logarithmically toward the IR, so the UV is the exposed flank). And for a ≲ 10⁻²⁶ m: ħc/a ≳ 2×10¹⁹ eV — which is the energy of **observed ultra-high-energy cosmic rays**. UHECR propagation phenomenology (no vacuum Cherenkov emission by protons over Mpc paths; no photon decay) bounds species cone-splittings at 10⁻¹⁹–10⁻²³ *at those energies* — i.e., the existing cosmic-ray data measure δ **essentially at the substrate cutoff**, pinning δ_UV ≲ 10⁻¹⁹. Insert into (14.3):

⟦rev [F-R14]⟧ **|Δc_GW/c| ∼ 10⁻¹⁹ as an order-of-magnitude estimate — GW170817's 10⁻¹⁵ bound is passed as an estimate, no longer "automatically by theorem": the four-orders margin stands numerically, but its theorem-grade guarantee is conditional on P-acoustic.** (14.4′)

The teaching narrative must now be rewritten — and the sharper lesson is the one the corpus's own method demands. The "methodological showcase" move — *when a killer bound arrives, derive the exposure's dependence structure before touching any dial* — was and remains **correct methodology**; keep it. What failed was not the move but a load-bearing sub-lemma inside it that was *asserted, not derived*: the positivity of every heat-kernel weight. The external audit did the derivation, and the assertion is false on the corpus's own action. So the showcase becomes the course's best cautionary pairing: the dependence-structure proof is only as strong as its weakest derived ingredient, and "each w_s is positive" was never derived — it was scenery painted to look like a wall. The professional sequence gains a fourth step: *derive the dependence structure; then audit each premise of the derivation to the same standard; only then call it a theorem; and price any rescue postulate openly* (P-acoustic's price is printed in the v3.0-ext paper). P-M3 — the definite-sign slaved Δc_GW prediction — is **suspended** pending P-acoustic: with a weight of undetermined admissibility, the sign is no longer definite. ⟦/rev⟧

**Weinberg–Witten, evaded by premises** [DF]. The famous theorem forbids massless spin-2 composites — isn't the induced graviton exactly that? Read the theorem's premises: it assumes *exact Lorentz invariance* and a *Lorentz-covariant conserved T^{μν}*. GUM's fundamental theory has neither (a preferred substrate frame; an emergent cone) — the theorem's hypotheses simply do not hold, which is the standard, legitimate evasion of the entire emergent-gravity class. **The teaching point outranks the physics point:** before citing a no-go theorem at a framework (or being cited at), *audit the premises against the framework's actual structure*. No-go theorems are precision instruments, and precision instruments used at the wrong workpiece cut nothing. (The residual honest flag: emergent diffeomorphism invariance is argued at derivation-sketch level — flag R-M3b, margin-protected because residual graviton-mass operators are (ka)²-suppressed far below the 10⁻²³ eV bound; the Ward-identity proof is ranked #2 in the paper's open-problems list, and this course does not pretend otherwise.)

## 14.4 The vacuum-energy bomb, and the Gibbs–Duhem defusal

**The nightmare at full strength.** A medium structured at a ≲ 10⁻²⁶ m carries zero-point energy density ∼ħc/a⁴ ⟦rev [F-T4-a §G5]⟧ ≳ 3.2×10⁷⁸ J/m³ (the previously printed 10⁹⁰ was an arithmetic slip caught by the external audit; evaluate ħc/a⁴ at a = 10⁻²⁶ m and check it yourself — the corrected figure is catastrophic enough) ⟦/rev⟧. Observed: ρ_Λ ≈ 6×10⁻¹⁰ J/m³. Mismatch: ≥10⁸⁷ — the corrected exponent changes nothing about the verdict; it is the same catastrophe-class as the literature's 10¹²⁰. This is the standard instant-death objection to *any* structured vacuum, and any course that buried it would be malpractice.

**The mechanism** [IM: Volovik-class; GUM-instantiated]. For a **self-sustained** medium — one whose ground state exists at *zero external pressure* (a droplet in vacuum, not a gas in a box) — ask what actually sources the induced gravitational equations. Answer: not the microscopic energy density ε_vac, but the **grand-potential density** ω_vac = ε_vac − Σμᵢnᵢ — and thermodynamics' Gibbs–Duhem identity forces ω_vac = −P_ext. Self-sustained means P_ext = 0. Therefore

**ω_vac = 0 in equilibrium — exactly, without fine-tuning.** (14.5)

The enormous zero-point ledger is real, but it is *chemical-potential bookkeeping* — energy paid for by the medium's own binding, invisible to gravity in equilibrium. Only **departures from equilibrium gravitate**. Two verifications inside GUM make this more than an imported slogan: (i) the down-payment — on the Bogomolny locus the topological sector's pressure P = ½Λ²b² − 𝒱 vanishes *pointwise* (Chapter 15's dust theorem): the compensation pattern operating natively in exactly the sector that dominates the ledger; (ii) the stress-test — the blue-fog vacuum's disclination web (Chapter 6) carries naive energy density ∼10²²ρ_Λ, a second heart-attack — defused by the *same clause*: the network **is** the ground state; it doesn't gravitate; only its transients do (the corpus's exhibit F-H2-1, a mechanism built for one catastrophe quietly absorbing another, which is the behavior of true structure rather than patchwork).

**The residual, with its sign theorem and shape** [DF + DW]. Departures gravitate — and the universe departs: Hubble expansion dilutes the substrate's conserved densities, dragging a lag δμ ∝ H/Γ in the slowest relaxing mode. Which mode? The corpus's answer is in-house: the **ε-lifted SDiff-soft manifold** — the same triple-duty flat directions of Chapter 6 (soft ⟹ parametrically slow Γ without tuning). ⟦rev [F-T4-a §G6]⟧ Expand about the self-sustained point — *in the right variable*. Earlier printings expanded ω(μ) ≈ ½χ⁻¹(μ−μ₀)² directly in the chemical potential, which silently **drops the linear term −n₀δμ** (ω′(μ) = −n ≠ 0 at the working point: μ is not the variable whose linear response vanishes). Teach the fix as a worked erratum, because it is exactly this course's own discipline — *check which variable's linear response vanishes before you expand quadratically*. The conserved density q (the mode's charge variable) is that variable: at the self-sustained point ∂ε/∂q = μ₀ and the Legendre structure gives, for a displacement δq,

**ρ_Λ = ½χ⁻¹δq² + … > 0: the sign of dark energy is guaranteed** (displacement energy from a stable minimum — the conclusion survives; the derivation is now in the variable that makes it true), (14.6′) ⟦/rev⟧

with magnitude ρ_Λ ∼ αH²M\*² — automatically of order the critical density (the coincidence problem addressed structurally: the residual *tracks* the expansion sourcing it). One pathology, stated openly because the corpus states it openly: a pure H² term tracks and cannot accelerate; acceleration is carried by the lag/memory term βḢ (linear response about matter domination gives β < 0), yielding the late-time equation of state

⟦rev [F-R12 — tier4-cosmo/wz_family_RESULTS.md]⟧ **w(z) = −1 + 𝔴·Ω_m(z), with the exact tracker identity at 𝔴 = 1: w(z) = −1 + Ω_m(z) — a freezing/tracker-class family, approaching −1 from above as structure growth dilutes, correlated with the growth history** [DW]. (14.7′)

(The old label "thawing" was a classification error — a family that starts near w > −1 at high z and *descends toward* −1 as Ω_m falls is freezing/tracker class; and in CPL coordinates the family sits at **w_a = +0.63𝔴 > 0**.) The previously printed sentence "The DESI-era preference (w₀ > −1, w_a < 0) sits on exactly this side of Λ" is **struck**: it was half right and the wrong half was load-bearing. The surveys' preferred direction matches the family in w₀ (> −1) and is the family's **kill direction in w_a** (the data lean w_a < 0, growth-anti-correlated at z ≳ 0.4; the family requires w_a > 0). Falsifiers both ways, unchanged in structure and now printed with their operative signs: (w₀, w_a) → (−1, 0) at 10⁻³ corners the family; a confirmed drift **anti**-correlated with growth (the w_a < 0 side) kills outright. ⟦/rev⟧ And one clause you must understand as governance, not physics: the same relaxation that lifts w above −1 *relaxes the cosmological neutrino-mass bound* — GUM thereby possesses a built-in defense of its Σm_ν stake (S1, Chapter 19), and the collaboration **pre-bound the escape hatch**: the extended-DE reading of the neutrino bound may be invoked in S1's defense *only if* the data independently prefer w(z) ≠ −1 at ≥3σ with the growth-correlated sign of (14.7). The rule was hashed before anyone knew which way it cuts. **Exercise 14.4 (★★, re-keyed in this edition).** ⟦rev [F-R12]⟧ A collaboration's stake is killed under ΛCDM but survives under w₀w_aCDM, and the same dataset prefers evolving DE at 2.4σ — *with the CPL lean on the w_a < 0 side*. ## 14.5 The foliation: emergent, near-CMC, Machian-dragged

The substrate's rest slicing defines a **dynamically emergent foliation F** — the simultaneity structure that Chapter 21's one-world dynamics requires and that relativity's textbook reading forbids as fundamental. Three properties reconcile the tension: (i) **law-like**: F = F(substrate state) — the theory is covariant with *contingent* structure, exactly as the CMB rest frame is contingent structure no one considers a violation of relativity; (ii) **near-CMC**: in cosmological regimes F coincides with uniform-density/constant-mean-curvature (York-time) slicing — converging, from material premises, on the slicing canonical quantum-gravity programs independently privilege, a consilience worth a raised eyebrow; (iii) **Machian-dragged**: the substrate participates in gravitational flow, so the laboratory's velocity relative to F is v_F = v_CMB − v_drag — predicting a **few-percent, direction-correlated offset** of any frame-detection experiment's recovered vector from the raw 370 km/s CMB dipole, testable against bulk-flow maps and triangulated against the anomalous quasar number-count dipole. That triangulation is Layer 2 of Chapter 23's experimental program — the sidereal probe whose 0.12 ns marker displacement is the frame's fingerprint — and the strong-field fine print is stated rather than hidden: CMC slicings pathologize inside horizons, which in GUM is the defect-dominated regime where the continuum description abdicates by the theory's own lights (open territory, honestly labeled R9-class).

# CHAPTER 15 — THE BOGOMOLNY MACHINE: THE KNOT MADE SOLVABLE

**Learning goals.** (1) Execute the Bogomolny completion and derive the topological energy bound. (2) Compute the Haar constant C₆ = (64/15π)Λm̃ by quadrature — twice. (3) Solve the saturating equation: the compacton. (4) Prove the three structural corollaries: SDiff flatness, additivity, dust. (5) Prove Derrick stability and derive the Yukawa tail and exterior multipole theorem. (6) Execute the scaling audit ε ∝ m̃^{−4/3} and watch it kill the shallow-knot neutrino (Teaching Case N-ν1).

## 15.1 The completion: energy bounded by topology

The topological sector's energy (Chapter 3): E₆₊₀ = ∫[½Λ²b_P² + 𝒱_tot(σ_P)]d³x, with b_P the topological density and deg P̃ = K protected by Lemma II.1. Now the oldest trick in soliton theory [IM: Bogomolny 1976], executed slowly. Complete the square:

E₆₊₀ = ∫ ½(Λb_P ∓ √(2𝒱_tot))² d³x ± Λ∫ b_P √(2𝒱_tot) d³x ≥ ± Λ∫ b_P √(2𝒱_tot) d³x. (15.1)

The first term is a perfect square, hence ≥ 0; the inequality saturates iff **Λb_P = ±√(2𝒱_tot)** pointwise — a *first-order* equation replacing the second-order Euler–Lagrange problem. The cross term looks like it depends on the profile — but it doesn't, and understanding why is the chapter's first insight. Recall Chapter 2's structural fact: b_P d³x is the degree-K pullback of the target S³'s normalized volume form. Therefore, for *any* function 𝒢 of position on the target (here 𝒢 = √(2𝒱_tot), a function of the "latitude" σ_P),

∫ b_P 𝒢(σ_P) d³x = K · ⟨𝒢⟩_{S³} — a spatial integral becomes a **group average**. (15.2)

Hence the bound: **E₆₊₀ ≥ C₆|K|, C₆ = Λ⟨√(2𝒱_tot)⟩_{Haar}** — energy bounded below by topology times a computable constant. Mass is winding number times a Haar quadrature.

## 15.2 The Haar constant, computed twice

**Quadrature one (the group average).** At c₂ = 0: 𝒱 = m̃²(1−σ_P), so √(2𝒱) = m̃√(2(1−cosχ)) = 2m̃ sin(χ/2) with σ_P = cosχ. The Haar measure on SU(2) in the latitude variable is (2/π)sin²χ dχ, χ ∈ [0, π]. Compute:

C₆ = Λ·(2/π)∫₀^π 2m̃ sin(χ/2)·sin²χ dχ = (4Λm̃/π)∫₀^π sin(χ/2)·4sin²(χ/2)cos²(χ/2) dχ. (15.3)

Substitute ξ = χ/2: = (16Λm̃/π)·2∫₀^{π/2} sin³ξ cos²ξ dξ = (32Λm̃/π)·(2/15) = **64Λm̃/(15π) = 1.3581 Λm̃.** (The beta-integral ∫₀^{π/2}sin³cos² = 2/15 — do it by u = cosξ as **Exercise 15.1 (★)**.)

**Quadrature two (the profile energy — the closed loop).** Solve the saturating equation on the hedgehog. With P̃ = e^{if(r)x̂·σ⃗}, the topological density is b = −(1/2π²)sin²f · f′/r²·(1/...)— use §2.3's form: b d³x = (1/2π²)sin²f|f′|dr dΩ, i.e., b = sin²f (−f′)/(2π²r²)·... Let us write the saturation condition directly in the latitude: Λ·sin²f(−f′)/(2π²r²)·(2π²-normalization bookkeeping — carry it carefully; the paper's App. B.1 form) = 2m̃ sin(f/2). Separate variables with the substitution s ≡ cos(f/2): the equation integrates in closed form to

**f₀(r) = 2 arccos(r/R\*), R\* = (2Λ/π²m̃)^{1/3}** — the *compacton*: f reaches exactly 0 at finite radius R\* (a hallmark of the sextic-plus-potential structure; the ε > 0 corrections smooth the edge into the Yukawa tail of §15.5). (15.4)

Now evaluate E₆₊₀ on (15.4) by direct integration — **Exercise 15.2 (★★)**, a page of careful calculus — and the answer is **exactly 64Λm̃/15π**: the group-average quadrature and the profile-energy quadrature agree. This "two computations, one number" pattern is not redundancy; it is the corpus's standing verification style, and you should adopt it: any result you can only get one way, you do not yet own.

## 15.3 Three structural corollaries (each one a load-bearer)

**(i) SDiff flatness** [DF]. Let ψ ∈ SDiff(ℝ³) (volume-preserving diffeomorphism). Then b_{P∘ψ} = (b_P∘ψ)·det Dψ = b_P∘ψ, and 𝒱(σ_{P∘ψ}) = 𝒱(σ_P)∘ψ — both energy densities merely *transport*: **E₆₊₀[P̃∘ψ] = E₆₊₀[P̃].** At ε = 0, volume-preserving reshaping and repositioning cost nothing. Three consequences already consumed elsewhere in this course, now finally proven: unit knots sit anywhere with zero force ⟹ **masses exactly additive** at leading order (Chapter 16's binding is an ε-effect); the flat manifold is the **dark-energy slow mode** (Chapter 14: parametrically soft ⟹ cosmologically slow relaxation, no tuning); and, ε-lifted, it is where the **chiral condensate lives** (Chapter 6's relocation). One degeneracy, three duties.

**(ii) Dust** [DF]. Anisotropic scaling x → (λ₁x₁, λ₂x₂, λ₃x₃) at the saturating solution: stationarity componentwise forces the on-shell stress tensor isotropic *and* the pressure

**P = ½Λ²b² − 𝒱 = 0 pointwise** on the Bogomolny locus. (15.5)

Near-BPS knot matter is *dust* — pressureless at leading order, P = O(ε) — the equation of state Chapter 14's cosmology uses, and the **down-payment on Gibbs–Duhem** (the compensation ε_vac ↔ pressure operating natively in the dominant sector).

**(iii) The binding expansion** [DW]. Turning on ε: M_K = C₆|K| + ε·m_K[1 + O(ε ln 1/ε)] — light binding, identity retention, composite structure, all as consequences of proximity to the BPS point. The benchmark binding fraction δ = 0.29 ± 0.05 ⟨r1⟩ is consistency-shaped, not a prediction (ε is a dial there).

## 15.4 Derrick stability, honestly

**Derrick's theorem** [IM], the graveyard of naive soliton models: under x → λx, an energy E = E₂ + E₄ + E₆ + E₀ (subscripts = number of derivatives; E₀ the potential) scales as λE₂ + λ⁻¹E₄ + λ⁻³E₆ + λ³E₀. Stationarity at λ = 1: **E₂ − E₄ − 3E₆ + 3E₀ = 0** (the virial identity — a mandatory check on every numerical profile; ⟨r1⟩ holds it to 3×10⁻⁴). Stability: the second λ-derivative at 1 must be positive: **2E₄ + 12E₆ + 6E₀ > 0** — manifestly true with all sectors present. The gradient-only theory (E₂ alone) has no stationary point at all — the collapse Chapter 3's flag F4 was installed to arrest, and now you see the arithmetic of the arrest: the quartic and sextic terms *gain* energy under shrinking exactly as fast as the quadratic loses it.

## 15.5 The tail and the exterior multipole theorem

Beyond the (smoothed) compacton edge, linearize: the profile decays on the gapped branch, f(r) → 𝔭·(1+μr)e^{−μr}/(μr)²·μ-normalized, with μ² = m̃²/(2a_ψ) — the Yukawa screening length of Chapter 10's Fact 2, and dipole constant **𝔭 = 0.84 ± 0.03 ⟨r1⟩** [CAL]. Now the theorem that makes the pair law of Chapter 16 tractable: **Theorem 15.2 (exterior multipole)** [DF]. The dressed hedgehog is invariant under *combined* spatial-plus-isospin inversion; this grading annihilates every even-ℓ exterior channel; and on the surviving ℓ = 1 block, Schur's lemma (the profile's residual symmetry acts irreducibly) forces the exterior to a **single scalar times a rigid frame**:

**φ^a(x) = p R_{ai}(Q) ∂_i(e^{−μr}/r), p ∝ 𝔭/μ².** (15.6)

At long range the knot *is* a Yukawa-screened orthogonal triplet of orientation dipoles — three dipoles locked at right angles, rotated rigidly by the knot's moduli frame Q. All of Chapter 16 is the interaction theory of these triplets. (Scope clause, learned the hard way in the corpus: the grading argument used the *dressed* hedgehog's symmetry; the undressed case needs its own classification — the clock-averaged spin-axis dipole of §15.6's fallout.)

## 15.6 Teaching Case N-ν1: the scaling audit that killed the shallow-knot neutrino

The corpus's most instructive execution, taught in the order it happened. **The temptation.** With mass = band-edge frequency, the neutrino looks free: give it a shallow edge, m̃_ν tuned so m_ν ∼ 0.05 eV, and everything else — spin-½, fermionic statistics, the closure — comes along by the universality theorems. The corpus built this sector. **The audit (step zero of the follow-up computation, and the step that mattered).** Before scattering amplitudes, *check the regime*: how does ε — the near-BPS parameter, ratio of (2+χ+4)-sector to topological-sector energy on the knot — scale with m̃? Count: E₂ ∼ a₂R\* (gradient energy over the core), E₆₊₀ = C₆ ∼ Λm̃, R\* ∼ (Λ/m̃)^{1/3}. Therefore

**ε(m̃) ∼ a₂R\*/(Λm̃) ∝ a₂ Λ^{−2/3} m̃^{−4/3} — ε grows as the mass falls, with power 4/3.** (15.7)

Insert the electron-to-neutrino mass ratio: ε_ν/ε_e = (m̃_e/m̃_ν)^{4/3} ≈ (10⁷)^{4/3} ≈ 2×10⁹. With ε_e ≤ 6×10⁻⁴ (Chapter 12's ceiling): **ε_ν ∼ 10⁶ — the shallow knot is not near-BPS; it is catastrophically deep in the quartic regime**, where the compacton picture, the closure constants, everything Chapters 10–12 built, is void. The claimed universality ("any smooth-core knot in any gap") had silently assumed ε ≪ 1 *per gap* — erratum E-A′-1, on the record. **The double execution.** In the quartic regime the knot is ordinary-Skyrme-stabilized: core R_Sk = √(a₄/a₂), mass **𝔪_Sk = c_Sk√(a₂a₄) ≈ 1.7 GeV — m̃-independent: knots cannot be parametrically lighter than the Skyrme scale** (the mass-floor theorem [DF-structural]; a 0.05 eV knot violates it by seven orders). Independently, the quartic-regime closure solves at its own pure number 𝔠_q = 3.1 ± 0.2 ⟨r5⟩ — excluded against the vacuum's ħ at 6σ. Two unrelated arguments, one verdict; cosmology (µm-tailed neutrinos would shred CMB free-streaming) arrives afterward as *corroboration, not executioner*. **The kill fired (N-ν1, pre-registered), the sector's predictions were retracted with cause, and the neutrino was reassigned** — to the collective sector, where Chapter 19 rebuilds it as something far stranger. The syllabus lesson, stated once and permanently: *before computing anything downstream of an ansatz, compute the ansatz's regime of validity as a function of every parameter you intend to vary.* Step zero is not preliminary; step zero is where theories die honestly instead of dishonestly.

# CHAPTER 16 — THE PAIR LAW, PHASE-LOCKED BONDS, AND KNOT MATTER

**Learning goals.** (1) Derive the screened-Hessian pair potential and the channel theorem (the π/⊥ rule). (2) Understand the calibrated bond and what "calibration zone" honestly means. (3) Derive clock coherence: why physical bonds are phase-locked Josephson pairs, and why like-species selectivity is derived. (4) Tour knot matter at template grade: FCC/Klein-four, homogenization, and the achirality gate's answer to the regress objection.

## 16.1 The screened-Hessian potential and the channel theorem

Two triplets (15.6) at separation X, relative body-frame orientation 𝒬 ≡ R(Q₁)ᵀR(Q₂). The linear-zone cross energy — each knot's triplet sampling the other's screened field — is (derivation: substitute both exteriors into the quadratic energy, integrate by parts twice; **Exercise 16.1 (★★)**):

**V_lin = C_d 𝒬_{ij} ℋ_{ij}(X), ℋ ≡ ∂∂(e^{−μr}/r): h_∥ = (2+2x+x²)e^{−x}/r³, h_⊥ = −(1+x)e^{−x}/r³ (x ≡ μr),** (16.1)

with the trace identity Tr ℋ = μ²e^{−μr}/r (the Yukawa equation — a built-in check ✓). **Channel theorem** [DF]: parametrize 𝒬 = R(θ, n̂) and expand: V = C_d[cosθ Trℋ + (1−cosθ)n̂ᵀℋn̂]. Scan the extrema: **global attraction at θ = π with n̂ ⊥ X̂** (V = −C_dh_∥ — the "π/⊥ rule": flip your partner's frame by a half-turn about an axis perpendicular to the bond); strong repulsion at θ = π, n̂ ∥ X̂; weak repulsion aligned. The attraction is monotone (no tail equilibrium); the bond forms where it meets the derived core repulsion, at **x₀ = 1.92 ± 0.08 ⟨r1⟩** — and here the course teaches the phrase **calibration zone** with full honesty: x₀ ≈ 2 is *not* deep in the asymptotic regime where (16.1) is exact; the corpus pre-registered this (kill N-Vb3′: the bond must sit at x ≥ 1.5 with mandatory calibration of the effective core constant), performed the calibration (𝔟_eff = 42 ± 6 versus asymptotic 𝔟_∞ ≈ 4.5 — a factor ~9, exactly the kind of moderate-separation correction the ancestor Skyrmion literature's methodology anticipates), and closed the loop: the bond equation at 𝔟 = 42 *returns* x₀ = 1.90 ± 0.05 ✓. ⟦rev [Group 3 credit — I.2 bond loop]⟧ **Margin note (dated 2026-07, external replication):** the bond loop was independently reconstructed end-to-end by the outside campaign, landing at **x₀ = 1.95 ± 0.07 — 0.3σ from the corpus's value**: the declared-calibration methodology this section preaches, paying off exactly as advertised. (The 𝔭/𝔟_eff conventions remain an OPEN disputed item per the charter; the loop closes under both parties' conventions.) ⟦/rev⟧ Asymptotic forms at moderate separation are *fitting forms* — a legitimate, validated methodology **when declared**, and fraud when not; the declaration is the whole difference.

## 16.2 Clock coherence: bonds are phase-locked clocks

Chapter 11 made every physical knot an isorotator. What does the mandatory clock do to (16.1)? Time-average the transverse dipole components, which rotate at ω: **(p-1)** For *unlike* species (ω₁ ≠ ω₂): ⟨cos(ω₁t+φ₁)cos(ω₂t+φ₂)⟩ = 0 — the transverse channels wash out; the surviving secular interaction is the spin-axis channel only (weaker, spin–spin anisotropic). **(p-2)** For *like* species (ω₁ = ω₂): the average is ½cos Δφ — the transverse attraction survives, **carrying a factor cos(Δφ) of the relative clock phase**: a Josephson-like, phase-dependent force. And the conjugate phase dynamics is a pendulum (𝕀_effΔφ̈ = −∂V/∂Δφ): like pairs **synchronize their Compton clocks**, the locked bond sitting at x₀^lock = 2.42 ± 0.12 ⟨r6⟩ (the ½-coherence factor shifts the frozen bond outward by ≈ln2/|d ln𝔟/dx| ≈ 0.7 — check the balance-equation logic as **Exercise 16.3 (★★)**; the measured shift 0.50 ± 0.14 agrees at ⟦rev [F-R8]⟧ 1.5–1.6σ (1.54σ in quadrature; 1.59σ on the ±0.14 alone — the previously printed "1.4σ" understated the pull, and a phrase like "graded honestly" must survive its own audit; the conclusion, agreement within ~1.5σ, is unchanged) ⟦/rev⟧), with a "bond vibron" libration mode as its spectroscopic signature. **(p-3)** Clock phases contribute U(1) dynamical holonomy only — statistics untouched (Chapter 17's no-braiding logic). Three consequences to file: **like-species selectivity of binding is derived** (unlike species barely bind — a structural fact, not an input); physical knot matter carries *two* order parameters (orientational + clock-phase XY, with a Josephson-plasmon branch); and the bench inherits a parameter-free *dynamical* stake — **S5: spontaneous clock phase-locking of like solitons, bond strength ∝ cos Δφ** — joining the static ratios ¼ and ⟦rev-consist [T3.1]⟧ κ²g_tot = 35/24 (S4′ as retargeted; the old 7/8 proved family-exact and unadjudicable — §23.5 revised) ⟦/rev-consist⟧.

## 16.3 Knot matter at template grade: the crystal, the loop, and the gate

First the scope declaration, because the corpus demoted this sector honestly and the course follows: at physical ε ≤ 6×10⁻⁴, fundamental knot–knot texture forces are negligible — the beautiful mathematics below governs *whatever composite level realizes it* (Chapter 20 finds one: the hadronic level) *and the analog platforms*, which live at benchmark ε by design. Template grade, labeled. Now the mathematics. **FCC/Klein-four** [DF]: with the four "colours" {𝟙, R_x(π), R_y(π), R_z(π)} (the Klein four-group in SO(3)) assigned to the four FCC sublattices, **every one of the twelve nearest-neighbour bonds of every site is compliant** — relative orientation a π-rotation about an axis ⊥ the bond, the attractive channel exactly (check three representative bonds as **Exercise 16.4 (★★)**); triangle cocycles close because the Klein group is abelian; and conversely, full compliance at coordination ≥ 8 forces FCC-with-this-colouring uniquely. **Homogenization — the Cosserat loop's first closure** [DF/CAL]: second-order expansion about the coloured crystal plus the continuum limit yields... an **Eringen micropolar medium** — composites of the substrate's knots form a medium *of the substrate's own constitutive class*, all moduli positive at the calibrated bond ⟨r1⟩. The ontology is self-similar in *form*. Which raises the regress objection — "turtles all the way down?" — and the corpus's answer is a theorem rather than a shrug: **the achirality gate** [DF]. The homogenized chiral modulus χ\* is a pseudoscalar bond sum, and the coloured crystal possesses a symmetry — mirror-through-cube-face composed with the induced Klein relabel — that *reverses every summand*: **χ\* = 0 identically. Chirality does not propagate up the loop by default** — each level's handedness is fresh constitutive data, the levels are not clones, and the regress (if the ontology even continues downward) is non-self-similar in exactly the respect that matters. Empirical shadow: nuclear matter is not optically active. **Exercise 16.5 (★★★).** Execute the gate: write χ\* as the sum over the twelve NN bond classes of the pseudoscalar invariant, apply the mirror-plus-relabel, and verify the pairwise cancellation.

# CHAPTER 17 — FERMIONS FROM TOPOLOGY: THEOREM c″

**Learning goals.** (1) Understand collective quantization and where the Finkelstein–Rubinstein signs live. (2) Make the belt trick rigorous: π₁ of configuration space and the exchange–rotation homotopy. (3) Understand the stratification problem — why the naive computation wasn't enough — and both proof routes of Theorem c″ at teaching depth. (4) Prove no-braiding (dressing cannot alter statistics) and the composite even–odd ladder.

## 17.1 Collective quantization and the FR sign

Quantize the knot's zero modes: position x, orientation Q̃ ∈ SU(2)-cover, phase α — wavefunctions on the moduli space, rigid-body Hamiltonian. The subtlety that decides everything: the *physical* configuration space 𝒞 identifies configurations related by symmetry, and wavefunctions need only be single-valued on 𝒞's **universal cover** — picking up a character χ(γ) ∈ {±1} of π₁(𝒞) along non-contractible loops γ [IM: Finkelstein–Rubinstein 1968]. If the 2π-rotation loop is non-contractible with χ = −1 available, the knot *may* be quantized as a fermion; if the exchange loop of two identical knots is homotopic to a 2π rotation of one (the belt trick's content), then **spin and statistics lock together**: χ_exch = χ_rot, and the fermionic choice is consistent. For textures valued in S³: π₁ of the one-knot configuration space is governed by π₄(S³) = ℤ₂ (loops of maps = maps from S³×S¹, whose relevant homotopy is π₄), and the classical computation (Williams 1970) puts the 2π-rotation loop at the *generator*, sign −1: knots are FR-fermions. So far, textbook [IM].

## 17.2 The stratification problem — the honest gap the corpus closed

The classical computation lives on the space of *smooth* charge-1 textures. But GUM's physical knot is **dressed**: it carries its disclination line (Chapter 13's charge), and the honest configuration space is *stratified* — smooth textures with an embedded line defect, the defect's own moduli included. Does π₄(S³)'s ℤ₂ survive the stratification, generator and sign intact? Or does the defect's presence open a path that contracts the 2π loop (bosonizing the electron), or add structure that alters the sign? For most of the corpus's history this was flag F-strat — the fermion sector resting on the unstratified precedent — and the red team correctly named it a foundation gap. ⟦rev [T-H4]⟦H4 resolved⟧ — theory-audit/h4_completion.md⟧ Earlier printings said **Theorem c″** "closed it, by two independent routes." This edition teaches the honest status instead: one route that *assumed what it needed at the decisive step*, one route whose printed sentence does not type-check, and a named computation that has now **settled the matter** — the ℤ₂ survives, order exactly 2, but not for the printed reason, and not with the printed group (see the resolved box below). That is a better lesson than the tidy one, and it is taught below in the corpus's own register. ⟦/rev⟧

⟦rev [T-H4]⟦H4 resolved⟧⟧ **Route 1 (fibration + obstruction theory), taught honestly.** Fiber the stratified space over the defect's moduli: base = configurations of the line ℓ; fiber F_ℓ = degree-1 textures on ℝ³∖ℓ with the prescribed meridian winding (the charge's boundary condition on a tube around the line). The route's *conclusion* — π₁(𝒞_strat) = ℤ₂ × A, "the constraint's contributions are purely abelian winding factors" — is exactly the step where the original text asserted what it needed. The honest statement carries a named hypothesis, the **minimal hypothesis MH**: that the connecting homomorphism ∂: π₂(ℝP²) = ℤ → π₁(F_ℓ) has **im ∂ ⊆ A**, plus splitness of the resulting extension. That connecting homomorphism is *not computed anywhere auditable* in the corpus, and the base has topology to spare — nothing cheap forces its image into the abelian factor. The three exact completions are the new teaching table, and you should be able to reproduce it cold: **(I) benign** — im ∂ ⊆ A and split: π₁ = ℤ₂ × A, the printed answer, fermions as advertised; **(II) ℤ₄-type** — the extension is non-split: "ℤ₂ × A" is *false* as printed, the rotation loop has order 4, and the statistics story changes character; **(III) fatal** — im ∂ hits the would-be ℤ₂: the sign is killed, the 2π loop contracts, and the closure system of Chapter 10 becomes *unsolvable* (no fermionic minimum exists to select). **Route 2 (Mayer–Vietoris), regraded to a teaching case in type-checking.** The printed sentence — decompose 𝒞 and "chase the connecting homomorphism: the rotation class lands in the same ℤ₂" — is *not type-correct in homology*: Mayer–Vietoris computes homology, the rotation class lives in π₁, and the π₁-correct tool (van Kampen) *has no connecting homomorphism to chase*. Read that sentence until you can say precisely why it fails to parse; that skill is the section's real syllabus. The H¹ steelman of Route 2 (abelianize and dualize) reduces, after the bookkeeping, to Route 1's link computation — so there was only ever **one** route, plus an echo.

**χ_exch = χ_rot(2π) = −1: identical dressed knots are fermions** — regraded [IM]-core + **closure corollary**: the undressed sign is Williams' classical computation [IM]; its survival under dressing rested on MH + the T-B1 selection + the App. F.3 lift, i.e. a corollary of the closure's consistency rather than an independent topological theorem — and MH's load-bearing clauses are now *computed true* ⟦H4 resolved⟧ (the rotation ℤ₂ survives, order exactly 2, χ(rot) = −1 at odd winding), with the F.3 exchange lift remaining the one honest gap. The old tag "[DF, twice-verified]" is struck — there were not two verifications; the two honest routes now on record are H4's own. (17.1′)

> **⟦H4 resolved⟧.** The stratified-completion computation H4 has **reported**: **∂(generator) = ±2·[rot_ℓ]** — the direction-sweep generator maps to *twice* the rotation loop — established by two independent routes (long-exact-sequence naturality to the principal SO(2) → SO(3) → S² bundle; an explicit hemisphere quaternion family whose SU(2) lift closes), 28/28 machine checks. Outcome, against the teaching table: **(III) fatal — REFUTED** twice over ([rot] ∉ ⟨2[rot]⟩ by parity); **(II) ℤ₄-type — REFUTED for odd meridian winding** (the elementary charge, w = ±1: χ(rot) = −1 exhibited splitness-free); **(I) benign — holds in corrected form**: im ∂ ⊆ A is true, but ∂ ≠ 0, so the printed answer needs its own correction — **π₁(𝒞_strat) = ℤ₂ × A/⟨2a_rot⟩**, not ℤ₂ × A: the winding factor acquires torsion, and the base's ℤ₂ does not survive at all for w ≠ 0 (a charged line cannot ride the orientation-reversing loop home). So (17.1′) upgrades with MH's clauses 1–2 discharged in-model; the casualties are "twice-verified" *and* the printed group (F-R16, minor); the pedagogical bonus is real physics: the defect line is a belt performing the belt trick — the 4π rotation is null-homotopic *through line sweeps*, which is exactly why ∂'s image is the even part. Chapters 2, 11, and 12's spin-statistics passages need no caveat. Still open, taught as such: the F.3 exchange lift (the two-knot space — why "ARE fermions" remains overreach); curved-line moduli; even w ≥ 2. [H4: theory-audit/h4_completion.md, F-R16]

⟦/rev⟧

*Undressed corollary* (consumed by Chapter 19's neutrino... with a twist): with no defect, π₁ = π₄(S³) = ℤ₂ directly — the classical case. *Pedagogical closing of the loop:* Chapter 11's spinorial clock (one period → −𝟙) and this chapter's exchange sign are the *same* ℤ₂ read dynamically and statistically — one topology, two manifestations, and the reason the closure system could demand a half-integer rung in Chapter 10 without circularity: ⟦rev [T-H4][T-H3]⟧ topology supplies *admissibility* — π₄(S³)'s ℤ₂ licenses the half-integer branch — and the closure supplies the *value* (j = ½, §12.4). The old sentence "the half-integer was forced here, before the closure ever ran" overclaimed in both directions: the forcing is the closure's, and the license's survival under dressing is the ⟦H4 resolved⟧ computation above — now computed, in corrected form, in the license's favor. ⟦/rev⟧

## 17.3 No-braiding, and the composite ladder

**No-braiding proposition** [DF]: in 3+1 dimensions, double exchanges are contractible, so the U(1) dressing (charge's compact phase, the clock phases of Chapter 16) contributes *dynamical holonomy only* — Aharonov–Bohm-type phases that shift energies and interference patterns but **cannot alter exchange statistics**. (Anyonic statistics is a 2+1-dimensional luxury; GUM's world has none.) Charge and statistics therefore never entangle — the electron is a fermion whether dressed or bare, and Chapter 16's clock-phase forces, however Josephson-like, are statistics-neutral (its p-3). **The composite ladder** [DF]: χ is multiplicative over constituents, so K-knot composites alternate fermion/boson with K's parity — the even–odd statistics ladder of nuclear physics, free of charge. The B = 2 worked example (the paper's App. F.5, set here as **Exercise 17.3 (★★★)**): enumerate the bond's symmetry group, compute the FR characters, and show J = 0 is *forbidden* while J = 1 with the internal-antisymmetric structure is the ground pattern — the deuteron's quantum numbers, emerging from homotopy theory and a bond geometry, the single most persuasive parlor trick the ancestor Skyrme program ever performed, and now GUM's inheritance.

Course-original: the "step zero" maxim of §15.6 and the two-ℤ₂-manifestations framing of §17.2; neither exceeds the corpus.

# CHAPTER 18 — THE FAMILY GENERATOR: FRUSTRATION AGAINST THE VACUUM'S TWIST

**Learning goals.** (1) Assemble the five requirements any family mechanism must satisfy — *before* proposing one — and understand why requirement-first construction is anti-numerology armor. (2) Understand helical frustration: families as relative-twist classes against the blue-fog pitch. (3) Derive the two-term structure Σ(p) = Ap + Bp(p−1)/2 by belt counting. (4) Execute Teaching Case E-F1 in full. (5) Read the ⟨r8⟩ confrontation table with the RT-8 grade locks binding. (6) Derive termination at three families and the forced Higgs nulls P-F1′.

## 18.1 Requirements first: the anti-numerology protocol

The corpus's family sector opens not with an idea but with a *cage* — five requirements assembled from already-proven theorems, each one eliminating a class of tempting mechanisms before anyone gets attached:

**R1 (only m̃ may vary).** Chapter 12's universality theorems lock Λ√J across species (else ħ fractures); the *only* per-species dial the closure tolerates is the gap parameter m̃. Any mechanism touching the stiffnesses is dead on arrival. **R2 (multiplicative composition).** The Higgs is the locking amplitude mode (Chapter 22), coupling as ∂M_k/∂m̃_c. Work the two candidate composition laws as **Exercise 18.1 (★★)**: multiplicative (m̃_k = η_k m̃_c) gives g_hkk ∝ M_k — the observed linear-in-mass pattern; additive-in-quadrature (m̃_k² = m̃_c² + Δ_k²) gives g_hkk ∝ m̃_c²/M_k — **inverse**-mass couplings, violently excluded by LHC data. Data have already voted on the composition law's algebra. **R3 (discreteness from topology).** Three families need an integer, not a continuum — winding numbers, not moduli. **R4 (no new elementary scalar).** Theorem 12.4 forbids knot-elementary scalars; a "familon field" would need its own non-knot construction and is disallowed as a free move. **R5 (hierarchy capacity).** The mechanism must span m_τ/m_e ≈ 3477 without per-level dials — exponential capacity from polynomial input.

*Why teach the cage before the mechanism?* Because the family problem is numerology's favorite honeypot — 207 and 3477 have been "derived" a hundred ways in the crank literature — and the only structural defense is to let *prior theorems* pre-commit the mechanism's shape. When the helical-frustration candidate arrives in §18.2, you should check it against R1–R5 as a reflex, and you should notice that R1–R5 were all in place, published, *before* the candidate was proposed. Order of operations is epistemics.

## 18.2 The mechanism: relative-twist classes

Chapter 6 built the vacuum: a blue-fog chiral condensate — locally helical at pitch q, globally isotropic. Now place a knot in it. The knot's asymptotic frame field must *match* the ambient helical texture, and the matching is classified by how many extra windings the knot's frame carries against the local pitch along the chiral axis: a **relative-twist integer p ∈ ℤ** — topological (R3 ✓), with laboratory ancestry (skyrmions in conical chiral magnets occupy exactly such discrete metastable twist classes; the FeGe literature is the anchor). Class-p frustration partially defeats the locking over the core region — misaligned belts where the knot's winding fights the vacuum's — and since locking dominates the gap (m̃² ≈ 4μ̃_c, Chapter 3), the effective edge is *multiplicatively* suppressed:

**m̃_p = m̃_c √s(p), s(p) = e^{−Σ(p)}** — multiplicative by mechanism (R2 ✓), with Σ(p) a frustration energy integral (R1 ✓: only m̃ moves), exponential in structure (R5 ✓), built from existing fields (R4 ✓). (18.1)

Family map: **p = 0 → τ (the unfrustrated base), p = 1 → μ, p = 2 → e** — all three *co-equal ground states of distinct topological classes* (not excitations of one another; the corpus polices this wording, and so will your examiners). Mass spacings: ln(m_p/m_{p+1}) = ½[Σ(p+1) − Σ(p)].

## 18.3 The two-term structure, by belt counting

What is Σ(p)? Count the frustration's anatomy. Each extra winding adds one **misalignment belt** — an annular region where the knot's frame shears against the vacuum's — costing a fixed energy: a term **A·p**, linear. And the belts *interact pairwise*: on the deep-BPS pancake (Chapter 12: the physical knot is extremely oblate) the belts are coplanar and strongly coupled, contributing **B·p(p−1)/2**. Hence

**Σ(p) = A p + B p(p−1)/2 ⟹ spacings ½(A + Bp) ⟹ spacing ratio R ≡ [ln(m_μ/m_e)]/[ln(m_τ/m_μ)] = (A+B)/A.** (18.2)

A *derived two-term shape with no free function* — the mechanism's entire flexibility is two computable integrals. Pre-registered kill **N-F1**, frozen before any computation: if the computed (A+B)/A falls outside [1.6, 2.2] (the band containing the data's 1.889 with method-honest width), the mechanism dies. Note the epistemic architecture: *the shape was staked before the integrals were evaluated.*

## 18.4 Teaching Case E-F1: the factor of two that hid inside an error bar

**⟨r7⟩, blind:** leading-order belt integrals gave A = 2.9 ± 0.6, B = 3.2 ± 0.9. Unblinding: ratio (A+B)/A = 2.10 ± 0.35 vs data 1.889 — **in band, N-F1 survived, the shape is right**. But the *magnitude*: ½A = 1.45 vs required 2.82 — short by ×1.9, absorbed at the time into "O(1) prefactor uncertainty of the pancake-background approximation." **One review cycle later**, a setup audit for the follow-up computation asked the question that should have been asked first: over which surface do the matching belts live? Answer: the oblate knot is a *pancake with two faces* — and the frame must match the ambient helix on **both** faces, with the two belt systems geometrically congruent and disjoint. **The frustration energy is exactly doubled: A, B → 2A, 2B. Erratum E-F1.** ⟦rev [F-R6]⟧ And now the case's **second act**, supplied by the external replication — because the corrected narrative you just read *itself* hid a chain that does not close. Run the printed chain numerically: 2.9 (the ⟨r7⟩ value) × 2 (E-F1) × 1.14 (the quoted cone stiffening) = **6.61, not 5.6** — and the printed error bars *shrink* (±0.6 → ±0.9 relative) exactly where the multiply-and-correct chain would force them to grow. The chain as narrated never produced (18.3). The corrected provenance, established by F-R6: **⟨r8⟩ was an independent re-evaluation of the belt integrals on the corrected two-face geometry** — not a patch-multiplication of ⟨r7⟩ — and E-F1's ×2 is confirmed *within* that re-evaluation; the "doubled, then corrected" story was a narrative compression that happened to be arithmetically false. The values: ⟦/rev⟧

**A = 5.6 ± 0.9, B = 5.7 ± 1.2.** (18.3)

Corrections are permitted; free corrections are not.

Now the locks, which are as much the syllabus as the table. **The self-grade:** the theory errors are ~15–20%; the data are known to 10⁻⁸; landing two O(1)-coefficient integrals on two targets at ≤0.5σ has prior weight ≈2–4% — the corpus grades the result **≈2σ-equivalent**, and the RT-8 language locks *bind*: externally this may be described only as "landing the hierarchy's logarithms at the O(1)-coefficient level" — never "derives the lepton masses," never "explains the hierarchy." **Exercise 18.3 (★).** A journalist's draft says "physicists derive electron and muon masses from first principles." ## 18.6 Termination and the forced nulls

**Why exactly three?** [DW-structural]. Ask what the p = 3 state would be: mass m₃ ≈ m_e·e^{−½(A+2B)} ≈ m_e·e^{−8.5} ≈ **100 eV** — and now run Chapter 15's scaling audit on it: ε₃ = ε_e·(m_e/m₃)^{4/3} ≈ ε_e·8×10⁴, which crosses the closure-failure boundary (ε ≳ 0.7, the quartic wall where 𝔠_q ≠ 𝔠) for all ε_e ≳ 8×10⁻⁶ — essentially the entire admission window of Chapter 12. **The tower terminates at three charged families because its fourth rung cannot close** — the same audit that killed the shallow-knot neutrino, now doing constructive work: one scaling law, one kill and one termination. **The forced nulls (P-F1′)** [DW]. Multiplicativity for the *electron* (p = 2, the deepest suppression) requires locking dominance to survive two frustration levels: m_V ≲ m̃_e. Propagate to the Higgs-coupling deficits δ_k = m_V²/(2m̃_k²): **δ_μ ≲ 1.2×10⁻⁵, δ_τ ≲ 4×10⁻⁸** — HL-LHC coupling measurements for μ, τ, b, t are predicted **SM-null at all foreseeable precision**; *any confirmed deviation there kills the mechanism* (stake S2); the electron Yukawa is the lone channel where an O(≤½) deficit may hide. Savor the epistemics: the mechanism's success (multiplicativity, forced by data via R2) *forces* a null prediction sharp enough to die by — the family generator is falsifiable at a collider that will run regardless.

# CHAPTER 19 — THE COLLECTIVE NEUTRINO AND STAKE S1

**Learning goals.** (1) Understand why the neutrino cannot be a knot (Chapter 15's kill) and what "collective" means as the alternative. (2) Build the heliknoton: Hopf charge in the vacuum's own order parameter; the pitch-quantum mass. (3) Price flag F12 honestly. (4) Understand Majorana genericity and the Majoron dictionary. (5) Follow the m_ν bridge — one computed logarithm — to stake S1, with the frozen-pipeline discipline as co-equal content.

## 19.1 From executed kill to reassignment

Chapter 15 left the neutrino homeless: not a knot (mass floor violated by 10⁷; quartic closure excluded at 6σ). But the *requirements* survive the corpse: something light (≪ 𝔪_Sk), chiral-channel resident (post-parity-violation), fermionic, Majorana-capable must exist. Where? The corpus's answer inverts the search: stop looking for a texture *in* the vacuum and ask what textures the vacuum's **own order parameter** supports. Chapter 6's blue fog has a long-wavelength order parameter — the transverse director n̂ ∈ S² (the cone of frames about the local pitch axis) — whose effective theory is an emergent Faddeev-class S² sigma model with a chirality-linear term. And S² has what S³ lacks: **π₃(S²) = ℤ — Hopf charge**, the linking number of preimage circles, a topological class the knot sector never had. The collective sector carries its own solitons.

## 19.2 The heliknoton: the neutrino as pitch quantum

The candidate [DW-structural]: the **unit-Hopf texture of the blue-fog director, stabilized by the ambient helix** — the *heliknoton*, an object with laboratory ancestry (cholesteric liquid crystals host exactly these: particle-like Hopf solitons whose size is set by the pitch, the Smalyukh-group experiments). Its properties, each derived: **Mass = pitch quantum.** Stabilization balances the chirality-linear gain against pitch-scale gradients: E ∼ γ_soft/q-structure ⟹ **m_ν ∼ ħqc**, with q = χ_soft/γ_soft (Chapter 6's Dzyaloshinskii pitch). Read the identity twice, because it is the sector's crown: *the smallness of the neutrino mass and the weakness of parity violation have one origin — χ is small.* One dial, two of nature's most famous smallnesses. (Inverted: m_ν ∼ 0.05 eV ⟹ pitch ∼ 4 µm — a mesoscopic vacuum scale, and the Vakulenko–Kapitanskii bound E ≥ c_VK Q_H^{3/4} guarantees topological stability *there*, legally evading the mass floor because the floor's premise — quartic-core stabilization — doesn't apply: the stabilizer is the background helix.) **Chiral residence** ✓ (a texture *of* the chiral sector: EM-dark, coupling only through the χ-channel — the weak interaction's home). **Fermionic — flag F12, priced in full.** For Hopf solitons, π₁(configuration space) = ℤ₂ (Krusch–Speight): fermionic quantization is **consistent but not forced** — contrast Theorem c″, where the knot's sign was a theorem. F12 is a discrete constitutive *choice* of the vacuum's quantization branch, and the corpus prints it as such: the neutrino's statistics costs one bit of postulate that the electron's did not. A course-long theme resolves here: know, for every fermion in your theory, whether its statistics is *derived or chosen* — GUM's answer differs by sector, and saying so is the honesty. **Majorana-generic** [DF-structural]. A Dirac mass is protected by a conserved U(1); the dressed knot had two (winding, compact phase); the heliknoton — uncharged, undressed — has **none**: nothing forbids the Q_H ↔ −Q_H connector, generic couplings generate the Majorana term, and 0νββ *must occur* at some rate (stake S6, conditional on this construction; full-funnel exclusion kills the sector). **Spectrum honesty:** two free-lunch mass towers were tried and killed in-memo — the Hopf tower E ∝ Q^{3/4} (predicts Δm² ratio 2.30 vs observed 32.6 ✗) and the same-η frustration tower (283 vs 32.6 ✗). Neutrino *multiplicity* remains input-grade, symmetric with the charged sector's A-M4 — the corpus derives neither family count from spectra, and says so in both places.

## 19.3 The Majoron dictionary: the vacuum's Goldstone, constrained

The blue fog's gapless phason couples to the heliknoton with strength **g = m_ν/f**, f ∈ [4, 29] MeV (Chapter 6's stiffness, in the operative ε-range): g ∈ [1.7×10⁻⁹, 1.3×10⁻⁸] — phenomenologically, **a Majoron model**, and the corpus imports that literature's constraint battery wholesale and passes it: free-streaming at recombination requires f ≥ 1.4 MeV ✓ (and *inverted*, this is the **cosmological floor B-ν1′: ε_e ≥ 1.5×10⁻⁶** — cosmology bracketing the near-BPS parameter from below while ħ-entrainment ceilings it from above: the corpus's recurring two-sided geometry); BBN ✓; supernova-cooling band evaded from below ✓; late recoupling harmless ✓. Two registered signatures with their honest conditions: ΔN_eff ≈ +0.027 [CJ-thermal — conditional on primordial phason population; a CMB-S4 null does not kill]; Majoron-mode 0νββ at g ∼ 10⁻⁹ [far-horizon].

## 19.4 The bridge and the stake: one logarithm to Σm_ν

Now the sector's decisive move — connecting the *charged* spacings to the *neutrino* mass through the mechanism they share. **Theorem 19.1 (the bridge)** [DF]. Chapter 18's linear coefficient A splits: A = A_core + A_far. The core term is q-blind (⟦rev [F-R7]⟧ qR\* ∼ 2.6×10⁻¹¹ — the previously printed 10⁻¹⁷ was a units slip caught by external recomputation; the corrected value is still eleven orders below unity, so the q-blindness argument is untouched ⟦/rev⟧ — the helix is locally uniform over any knot core), but the p-winding's mismatch *relaxes over the pitch length outside the core*, and line-defect twist relaxation in a chiral medium is **logarithmic**:

**A(q) = A_core + κ_far ln(1/qR\*).** The measured A is a *pitch gauge.* (19.1)

**The frozen pipeline** [the discipline, co-equal content]. Before the precision computation ran, an audit froze every discretionary choice: base scale = m̃_τ (the unfrustrated family — canonical, no alternative admitted); R\* = R\*(τ); full (A_core, κ_far) covariance to be published; the kill band N-F2 = [10⁻³, 0.3] eV re-affirmed verbatim; hashes lodged. Then ⟨r10⟩ at ±3% precision: **A_core = 3.05 ± 0.09, κ_far = 0.1065 ± 0.0032** ⟦rev [F-R3]⟧ — with the covariance annotated in this edition: the published ±0.90 band on (19.2) corresponds to an *effective* correlation **ρ_eff = −0.36**; the previously printed "ρ = +0.45" was a sign/convention slip (it does not reproduce the band), corrected without any change to the band or the prediction ⟦/rev⟧ — with the center *holding* under a sixfold error shrink from the exploratory run (a nontrivial stability pass: it was free to drift by ±8 and moved by 0.04). Invert with the data-side A = 2ln(m_τ/m_μ) = 5.644:

**ln(1/qR\*) = 24.36 ± 0.90 ⟹ m₃ = m̃_τ e^{−24.36±0.90} = 0.047 eV, 1σ band [0.019, 0.115] eV.** (19.2)

N-F2 survived. Intersect with the oscillation floor (√Δm²₃₁ = 0.0503 eV — the heaviest state can't be lighter than its splitting) and the cosmological Σ-bound: live window m₃ ∈ [0.050, 0.057] eV, the prediction sitting 6% *below* the floor — a 0.08σ pull. Convert to the observable cosmology measures:

**STAKE S1: Σm_ν ∈ [0.058, 0.11] eV, normal ordering. KILL: any robust cosmological determination Σm_ν < 0.058 eV.** Adjudicators: DESI-DR3/Euclid — *scheduled, dated, external.* (19.3)

**The grade, and why it is the syllabus.** The target (0.05 eV) was world-public — no blinding of the *comparison* was possible; the discipline therefore lives entirely in the **frozen pipeline** (no dial existed to turn toward the answer), and the evidential weight is assessed accordingly: [DW, magnitude-consistent, center-stable], standalone **≈2σ-equivalent** — a *stake*, not a claim, and the collaboration's response to every possible verdict (kill, survival, the evolving-DE complication with its pre-bound escape hatch, inverted ordering, data pathology) was hashed *before the data* in the standing-watch protocol you drilled in Exercise 14.4. **Exercise 19.2 (★★).** DESI-DR3 reports Σm_ν = 0.071 ± 0.015 eV, NO preferred. Walk SWP Branch S: what may the collaboration say ("the stake survived its adjudicator" — the ceiling), what must it do before saying anything stronger (external replication of the ⟨r10⟩ pipeline), and what remains forbidden (the word "confirmed"). **Exercise 19.3 (★★★, restated in this edition).** ⟦rev [F-R3]⟧ The original exercise — "propagate (δA_core, δκ_far, ρ = +0.45) through the inversion, verify the ±0.90" — was **unsolvable as stated**: propagating ρ = +0.45 literally does not return ±0.90, which is precisely how the external replication caught the covariance slip. Restated: (a) propagate the corrected covariance (ρ_eff = −0.36) and verify the ±0.90; (b) then do the discovery exercise the original accidentally was — propagate the printed +0.45, obtain the wrong band, and identify from the mismatch alone which sign convention must have been flipped (the external replication's WS-nu-P2-class recomputation is your answer key). ⟦/rev⟧ ⟦rev [Group 3 credit]⟧ **Margin note:** the entire bridge arithmetic — logarithm, mass, band, Σ-window — is externally replicated (Tier 0 + WS-nu-P2-class recomputation); S1 and the frozen-pipeline discipline narrative stand unchanged. ⟦/rev⟧

# CHAPTER 20 — CONFINEMENT: THE VACUUM'S CENSORSHIP

**Learning goals.** (1) Derive confinement as vacuum-web topology: fractional winding must terminate network lines. (2) Use the stratum trichotomy to dissolve the tension-gap crisis — a case study in scale misassignment. (3) Prove the confinement dichotomy: charge free ⟺ photon massless; color confined ⟺ stratum gapped. (4) Understand closure censorship: why all six quarks are confined regardless of mass. (5) See the Cosserat loop close a second time: hadrons ARE the emergent Skyrme model. (6) Read the quark-spacing results, sign flip included, at their honest grade.

## 20.1 Confinement by subpoena: fractional winding and the web

Chapter 6's vacuum is not empty: it is the blue fog — double-twist regions stitched by a **disclination network**. Now place in it a knot carrying *fractional* frame winding relative to the network's texture (the color-charge ansatz; the fraction is 2π/n with n the network's junction order — n = 3 the baryonic case, with the junction-order computation WS1-H3 carrying a pre-registered kill: a robustly 4-fold network would predict tetraquark-like ground-state baryons, excluded). A fractional winding **cannot be screened by smooth fields** — no continuous texture interpolates a third of a turn to closure — so the knot must *terminate a network disclination line*: it is topologically subpoenaed to the web. Line tension × length ⟹ **linear potential**; finite-energy states are exactly the **line-neutral composites**: knot–antiknot pairs (mesons) and n-knot junction closures (baryons). Confinement is not a force that grows; it is a *tether that cannot be cut* — the dual-superconductor picture of QCD lore, realized materially, with the Y-law three-quark geometry (genuine central junction, favored by lattice QCD at long distance) as a banked structural consistency.

## 20.2 Teaching Case F-Q1: the tension gap, dissolved by trichotomy

**The crisis as first computed.** QCD's string tension: σ ≈ (440 MeV)² = 0.19 GeV². The soft-sector phason strings of Chapter 6: T ∼ f² ∈ [1.6×10⁻⁵, ~10⁻³] GeV² — short by **×50 to ×10⁴**. For one review cycle this stood as the color sector's named wall: the confining string seemed energetically impossible. **The dissolution — by asking which stratum.** The theory's line defects come in *three* tension classes, set by which sector's stiffness the line's core breaks [DF]: **T1** — fundamental-texture disclinations, T ∼ substrate scale (≳(10¹⁰ GeV)²: cosmologically absent, and must be — the W-net watch); **T2** — flux tubes of a *gapped* orientation stratum, T ∼ M_gap² of that stratum; **T3** — soft-sector phason strings, the f² class. The crisis assumed confining strings were T3. They are **T2**: the color winding couples to the gapped locking stratum, and a gapped stratum's flux *cannot spread* — the tube's tension is set by the quark-sector gap scale, σ = πf_q²ln κ_q, giving f_q ≈ 0.14–0.20 GeV by inversion. The ×10⁴ gap was a **stratum misassignment**, and the transferable lesson is blunt: *in a multi-sector theory, every dimensional estimate must carry its sector label* — "the string tension" was never one quantity; it was three, and the crisis was a pronoun error.

## 20.3 The dichotomy and the censorship

**Theorem 20.1 (confinement dichotomy)** [DF]. A winding charge confines **iff its mediator stratum is gapped**: gapless mediator (B2 — the photon, exactly massless by Theorem II.2) ⟹ flux spreads, elliptic 1/r² far field ⟹ *electric charge is unconfined*; gapped mediator (the locking stratum) ⟹ flux tubes ⟹ *color is confined*. **One gap structure, both experimental signs** — the photon's masslessness and the quark's imprisonment are two faces of the same spectral fact, and Chapter 4's organizing sentence ("photon and matter are two projections of one field") acquires its sharpest consequence. ∎

**Theorem 20.2 (closure censorship — the repaired form)** [DF]. Fractional winding is *one cause with two consequences*. Consequence one: the tube (20.1). Consequence two: the winding's dressing carries a **mass-blind sector floor** ε_dress = (f_q/𝔪_Sk)² ≈ 10⁻² — *twenty-fold above the entrainment ceiling* of Chapter 12: no fractionally-wound species can close as an asymptotic stationary state at the vacuum's ħ. And confinement is exactly what removes the obligation: *only asymptotic states owe closure*, and the only asymptotic states are the line-neutral composites — which close at the emergent level. **All six quarks — top included — are censored and confined; all leptons are free: the observed dichotomy, exact and universal across quark masses.** ∎ (The "repaired form" flags a real corpus error worth two sentences: the first draft read the *species* ε(m̃) against the ceiling, which would have set the heavy top *free* — the audit F-A14-1 caught the conflation of the mass-scaling ε with the winding's dressing floor, and the repair made the theorem *stronger*: the floor is mass-blind, so the dichotomy is exact. Language lock F-A14-2, binding: never say "quarks violate ħ-consistency" — confined constituents are internal moduli of a closing composite; only asymptotic states owe the closure anything.)

## 20.4 The loop closes twice: hadrons are the emergent Skyrme model

The long-wavelength theory of the line-neutral composites is the effective theory of the locking-stratum orientation field: stiffness f_q ≈ 0.14–0.20 GeV, stabilizing quartic — **a Skyrme model with f in the f_π range**. Pause and hear what that says: the ancestor framework this whole course has been importing methodology from — the hadronic Skyrme model, with its FCC nuclear matter, its FR-fermionic baryons, its B = 2 deuteron — is identified as **GUM's own L2 effective theory of the color sector**. The Cosserat loop (Chapter 16: knot composites form a medium of the substrate's own class) closes a *second* time, now with the hadronic scale as the seam, and every borrowed Skyrmion result becomes an in-house prediction one level up. The honest residue, printed as the paper prints it: f_q is *inverted from σ*, not derived — deriving ε_q ≈ 10⁻² from substrate structure is the sector's wall (ranked #1 in the program's open problems), and the "c_T ≈ 1.6 = O(1)" consistency between σ and the constituent scale carries a mandatory hygiene clause: *dimensional consistency between two quantities that are one scale in QCD is not a prediction.*

## 20.5 The quark spacings: the sign flip

Can the family generator (Chapter 18) run in the quark sector? Not verbatim — the tube changes the belts. At ε_q ∼ 10⁻² the knot is only moderately oblate (λ\* = 0.25 ± 0.03): the belts are **non-coplanar**, collapsing the geometric pairwise term (B_geo/A → 0.33 ± 0.08); and the confining flux threading the core splits the classes by tube alignment ζ = ±1: aligned windings get weak pairwise screening (B_tube/A = 0.06 ± 0.04), anti-aligned get strong (0.51 ± 0.14). The ⟨r11⟩ confrontation: aligned class → spacing ratio R = 1.27 ± 0.09 vs up-type data 1.30 (0.3σ), with A = 9.4 ± 1.9 vs 9.82; anti-aligned → **R = 0.82 ± 0.16 vs down-type 0.79** (0.2σ), with A = 7.9 ± 1.8 vs 7.60 — four numbers at 0.2–0.3σ, **including a derived sign flip**: the down-type's *decreasing* spacings come from a negative effective B (tube screening overwhelming the collapsed geometric term) — a structure the lepton mechanism could not produce at any parameter values, which is exactly what makes it evidential. Penalties printed with the result: the up/down ↔ aligned/anti-aligned assignment carries a ℤ₂ freedom (½ prior penalty); quark *termination* is not derived (censored species never individually close — the terminator has no purchase); base scales (m̃_t, m̃_b) are inputs. Combined grade: **≈2σ-equivalent, assignment-penalized** — the same shelf as the lepton result, and the sector's honest final sentence is the paper's: the family mechanism now *lands shapes* in both sectors and *derives* neither sector's count. ⟦rev [Group 3 credit — ⟨r11⟩]⟧ **Margin note (dated 2026-07, external replication):** the ⟨r11⟩ table was reproduced by the outside campaign *to the error digits*, with the sign flip **forced** (their independent evaluation could not produce the down-type's decreasing spacings any other way) and the m_t scheme pinned in the process — teachable as the payoff of the declared-calibration methodology this section preaches: because every choice was declared, an outsider could re-run all of them. ⟦/rev⟧

# CHAPTER 21 — ONE WORLD: THE TAX, THE LEAVES, THE PADLOCKS, AND THE GATE

**Learning goals.** (1) Prove that Bell's tax is *conserved* — the exponential cost of realism cannot be evaded, only re-addressed — via Spreeuw's and Bédard's theorems at teaching depth. (2) Understand hypersurface Bohm–Dirac dynamics and the elliptic mechanization of entanglement. (3) Prove both padlocks: Valentini's signal-locality theorem and elliptic silence. (4) Understand the unlock — what non-equilibrium would buy — and the standing inverse-kill. (5) Master T-KILL: the theorem, the surviving mechanism, and the A3 gate that routes a century-old metaphysical question through one measurable spin cutoff.

## 21.1 Bell's tax is conserved: the three payment addresses

Bell's theorem is not on trial in this course — loophole-free experiments closed that file in 2015 — but its *invoice* is widely misread, and the corpus's Chapter-zero move is to prove the invoice is conservation-law-shaped: every realist reading of quantum mechanics pays an exponential, and the only freedom is the payment address.

**Address 1: the arena (orthodoxy/Bohm-on-configuration-space).** Ψ lives on ℝ^{3N} — the exponential is paid in the *dimension of the arena*, and 3-space realism is surrendered at the door.

**Address 2: mode count (classical 3-space waves).** Could a classical field in ordinary space carry quantum information? **Spreeuw's theorem** [IM], taught by construction: classical optics *does* realize single-qubit structure — polarization + two paths = "cebits," with gates and interference — but entangling N cebits classically requires *physically multiplying the modes*: every basis state of the N-qubit space needs its own beam. Count: N qubits ⟹ 2^N beams. Diffraction-limits the beam density, the observable universe limits the volume, and the arithmetic lands near **N ≈ 214 qubits to fill the cosmos**. Classical 3-space waves pay in *exponential mode count* — and the payment is not an artifact of optics but of the tensor-product structure itself. **Exercise 21.1 (★★).** Reconstruct the 214: beams at diffraction limit λ³-packed, λ ∼ 500 nm, Hubble volume; show the answer is logarithmically insensitive to every input — which is why the theorem is a wall, not an estimate.

**Address 3: local state dimension (local hidden fields).** Could local fields at each point carry the load? **Deutsch–Hayden descriptors** do exactly this for quantum theory itself — perfectly local bookkeeping — and **Bédard's cost theorem** [IM] prices it: the local descriptor's dimension is *exponential in N*. Locality is preserved; the exponential moves into each point's state space.

**The conservation law, and GUM's chosen address.** Arena dimension, mode count, or local state dimension: the exponential appears at exactly one address in every known realist accounting, and GUM's structural claim [DF-class bookkeeping] is that this is forced — Bell correlations *are* that much information, and ontology only chooses where to warehouse it. GUM pays at Address 3, in a specific currency: the **Norsen tower** of local moment fields (Chapter 9's Layer 2) — the conditional wave function plus its partner-direction derivative fields, exponential in principle. And then it discovers the discount that makes the address livable ⟨r1⟩: for **area-law entanglement** — essentially every physically prepared state — the tower truncates at depth 3–5, N-independently; the exponential is spent only on genuinely volumetric entanglement (GHZ-class), which is rare, fragile, and deliberately manufactured. *The tax is real; the typical bill is small.* The honest seminar sentence, verbatim from Chapter 9 and now fully backed: GUM does not make entanglement local — it makes entanglement's *bookkeeping* local and its *coordination* leaf-instantaneous, pays the provable exponential in field content, and finds that physical states are a few fields deep.

## 21.2 Leaves and the elliptic mechanization

**The dynamics** [IM: hypersurface Bohm–Dirac]. N-knot guidance proceeds on the leaves Σ of the substrate foliation F (Chapter 14's emergent, Machian-dragged rest slicing): a multi-time wave function evaluated on leaves; each knot guided by the leaf-normal-contracted current; **leaf-wise equivariance** — |Ψ_Σ|²-distributions transported to |Ψ_Σ|²-distributions — holding by construction. GUM's three additions to the imported skeleton: F is *supplied* by the medium, not posited (the standard embarrassment of HBD theories — "whose foliation?" — answered by ontology); the guidance law is itself an *attractor* (wake drag, ∼1/ω₀ — Chapter 8's mechanized postulate); and the current's dependence on all N positions is **mechanized** rather than mysterious:

**The elliptic mechanization** [DF-construction]. In the stiff-compression limit, branch B1 (Chapter 4) degenerates into a leaf-wise elliptic constraint — ∇²p = −s[x; X₁,…,X_N, texture] — solved *instantaneously-in-leaf* (physically: at c_L ≫ c, floored at c_L/c ≳ 10⁴ by Bell-timing baselines), with each knot's guidance reading the local gradient of the global solution. **Entanglement is a shared pressure boundary-value problem**: knot i's wake "knows" about partner j's position the way the water level in one arm of a U-tube knows about the other — through a constraint, not a signal. This is channel (b) of Chapter 13's separation (pressure, sourced by cores) — never to be confused with channel (a) (frame connection, sourced by winding) — and its equivalence to configuration-space Bohmian dynamics is verified numerically at **8×10⁻⁴ RMS** (3D two-knot EPR, tower depth 5) ⟨r1⟩ [CAL]: the mechanization is not a metaphor; it integrates.

Stat.
