## 21.3 The double padlock

Realist nonlocality plus a preferred foliation is a loaded gun: why can't Alice signal Bob? The corpus's answer is two theorems that turn out to be one fact.

**Lock 1 (no signalling — Valentini's theorem)** [IM, premises verified in-house]. In equivariant pilot-wave dynamics, the *marginal* statistics at B are independent of spacelike operations at A **if and only if ρ = |ψ|²** — signal-locality is exactly quantum equilibrium, no more, no less. GUM verifies the premises rather than citing them: leaf-wise equivariance ✓ (construction); equilibrium ✓ with *rates* (Chapter 8's H-theorem plus the ⟨V-SIM-4⟩ relaxation dynamics); and the reflexive clause that makes the lock a padlock — **the agents are made of the medium**: any instrument that would aim the superluminal channel is itself an equilibrium ensemble, its pointer statistics Born-distributed, its aim therefore randomized by exactly the distribution that closes the channel. The padlock self-applies.

**Lock 2 (no energy — elliptic silence)** [DF]. Independently: the constraint sector *transports no energy for any source motion whatsoever*. Proof shape (the paper's App. E.5, teachable in three sentences): radiation requires a pole surface in the propagator — a locus ω(k) where real waves carry flux to infinity; the elliptic sector's propagator is −1/k², **pole-free**: however violently the sources dance, the constraint field re-solves and nothing propagates. Finite-c_L correction: leading (quadrupole) leakage scales as (c/c_L)⁵ ≤ 10⁻²⁰ at the Bell floor ⟦rev [T4-W3 / F-T4-6]⟧ — with this edition's multipole caveat, per the paper's revised Lock-2 entry: the ⁵ exponent is the *quadrupole* channel's; the general-multipole accounting has not been closed, and the conservative fallback is the dipole-class **(c/c_L)³ ≤ 10⁻¹²** — still eleven orders below anything the Bell floor could see, so the lock's conclusion is untouched while its stated exponent is honest ⟦/rev⟧. (The three-sentence pole-free core above is exactly as before; it is the correction term, not the theorem, that carried the flag.)

⟦T6⟧ **Now executed — the Lock-2 conservation lemma, at toy grade (Tier-6, dated 2026-07).** [F-T6-5-EXEC; T4-W3 toy discharge; 5/5 gates PASS] The multipole caveat in the ⟦rev⟧ note above now has a runnable exhibit and a lemma. Exact retarded solutions, 3-D scalar wave equation: measured radiated-power exponents in 1/c_L = **1.000000** (non-conserved monopole source), **2.999844** (charge-conserving, momentum-violating dipole), **4.999869** (charge- and momentum-conserving quadrupole); flux R-independence 2.5×10⁻⁴. Lemma (proof-sketched): order-ℓ far-field amplitude ∝ (ωa/c_L)^ℓ; charge conservation kills ℓ = 0, momentum conservation kills ℓ = 1 ⇒ leading leakage quadrupolar, (c/c_L)⁵. Consequence for the paragraph above: the T4-audit fallback (c/c_L)³ ≤ 10⁻¹² and the printed (c/c_L)⁵ ≤ 10⁻²⁰ are now separated by ONE named, isolated assumption — *the Lock-2 constraint source is built from locally conserved knot mass/momentum densities*. Proposed regrade (annotation #4, a proposal — adoption is the authors'): Lock 2 [DF core + GAP-minor] → [DF core + conservation-lemma-conditional], field-level verification of the source form = the remaining sharply posed obligation. Toy-grade binding: scalar wave equation, prescribed sources. Within-model. Artifact: tier6-foundations/L3/RESULTS.md.

**One lock, two faces** [DF]. The two theorems are the same structural fact read twice: *an elliptic constraint is a solver, not a channel.* It coordinates (that is Bell's correlations) without transporting (that is no-signalling and no-energy). The superluminal sector of GUM is exactly as usable as the constraint that keeps a U-tube level: infinitely fast, perfectly silent.

**Radiation interlude (the two companion theorems, owed since Chapter 11).** The same propagator analysis, run on the *gapped* sector, delivers the pair that disciplined the closure: **(VIII.D.1, no runaways)** — the gapped response kernel is even-analytic below the cut: no ω³ (Abraham–Lorentz) term exists, self-force is conservative, the century-old pathology of point-charge dynamics is structurally absent; **(VIII.D.2, silence and the superelastic window)** — below-threshold texture dynamics is *exactly* non-radiating (evanescent exterior, zero flux); above ω₀, knot bremsstrahlung opens with the massive-channel √-onset. VIII.D.2 is the theorem that vetoed the superradiant rung (Chapter 11's κ = √(7/6) rejection) and scripts the bench's re-entry protocol (Chapter 23): over-spin a soliton and *watch* it shed angular momentum until κ < 1.

## 21.4 The unlock, priced

What would non-equilibrium buy? Parametrize ρ = |ψ|²(1 + εf): entangled pairs carry superluminal channel capacity **C ≈ κ²ε² bits/pair** [DW]. At relic depth ε ∼ 10⁻⁶ and 10⁹ pairs/s: ∼10⁻³ bit/s — *detection-grade, not communication-grade* (a statistical anomaly hunter's signal, not an ansible); at ε ∼ 10⁻² it would be an ansible, which is one of several reasons the corpus flags relic-non-equilibrium claims [CJ] with non-claim discipline. And the standing inverse-kill, as clean as they come: **C ≡ 0 at equilibrium is a theorem here — any confirmed superluminal signalling *without* accompanying Born-rule violation falsifies this architecture outright.** GUM is the rare framework that would be killed by a communication miracle.

## 21.5 T-KILL and the A3 gate: metaphysics routed through a spin cutoff

Now the sector's masterpiece of experimental logic. The foliation is real in GUM — there is a *fact* about which of two spacelike measurements happened first. Is that fact detectable? The corpus pre-registered a kill test (T-KILL) and executed it *both ways*:

**Theorem 21.1 (T-KILL fires for POVM detectors)** [DF]. If Bob's arrival-time detector is modeled by the absorbing-boundary rule — a genuine POVM E_B(dt) on Bob's Hilbert factor — then for any spacelike operation by Alice, the coincidence statistics Tr[ρ(E_A ⊗ E_B(dt))] are **exactly order-independent**: local CP maps on distinct tensor factors commute, period. Under POVM modeling, no foliation probe exists even in principle. (Numerically confirmed to 2×10⁻⁵ in 3D ⟨r1⟩.) ∎

**Proposition 21.2 (the mechanism survives for non-POVM statistics)** [DF+CAL]. *First-crossing* trajectory statistics — "when did the particle's actual trajectory first cross the detector plane" — are functionals of the **conditional wave function**, hence of Alice's F-time, and are provably *not* a POVM on Bob's factor. For these, coincidence order-dependence survives: **9.6% peak in the near field** ⟨r1⟩, decaying far-field. ∎

**The gate** [DF] — and read this logical chain until it is reflexive, because it is the finest piece of experimental epistemology in the corpus: Chapter 23's Layer-1 experiment measures whether nature exhibits the **A3 signature** — the spin-orientation-dependent hard support cutoff in arrival times that trajectory theory predicts and every POVM proposal forbids. Then: ⟦rev [T4-W5 / F-T4-7]⟧ **A3 observed ⟸ would be entailed by a non-POVM arrival observable — but the printed converse carried a named gap, and this edition weakens the first ⟺ accordingly: observed A3 refutes every POVM *proposal on record* (absorbing-boundary and its published kin), not the POVM *class*. Hard-support POVMs exist — construct one as a check: a time-gated detector whose POVM density E_B(dt) is built with explicit support cutoff t ≥ t_gate(spin) is a perfectly good POVM exhibiting an A3-like cutoff. The missing theorem — that no POVM on Bob's factor can reproduce the full spin-correlated A3 signature — is the gate's own standing debt, printed here as such; until it is proven, the honest chain is: A3 observed ⟹ every proposal on record fails ⟹ the foliation probe's mechanism is live-by-default ⟹ fund Layer 2, with the all-POVM exclusion theorem owed before "non-POVM statistics are real" may be asserted.** Exercise 21.4 below — which already walks exactly the right structure for Prop. 21.2 — gains part (c): attempt the all-POVM exclusion for the spin-correlated cutoff and identify precisely where the attempt sticks. ⟦/rev⟧ **A3 absent with absorbing-boundary statistics confirmed ⟹ arrival time is a POVM in practice ⟹ T-KILL fires ⟹ Layer 2 is retired *by theorem*, with no hardware wasted.** The question "is there a fact about which measurement happened first?" — a century of interpretation-war ammunition — is routed, in its entirety, through **one measurable spin cutoff in a cold-atom time-of-flight distribution.** Either answer is information; neither is rhetoric. **Exercise 21.3 (★★).** Prove the commutation step of Theorem 21.1 explicitly for E_A a projective spin measurement and E_B(dt) an arbitrary POVM density on the other factor. **Exercise 21.4 (★★★).** Explain, in one page, why first-crossing statistics fail to be a POVM — construct the two preparations of Bob's *reduced* state that are identical as density matrices yet yield different first-crossing distributions when Alice's conditioning differs, and identify exactly which premise of the POVM formalism breaks.

⟦T6⟧ **Now executed — a POVM-exclusion testbed for the gate's printed debt (Tier-6, dated 2026-07; [DW, testbed-grade, configuration-specific]).** [F-T6-L7a/b] The all-POVM exclusion owed in the revised chain above now has a constructive candidate; students should first master its operational schema (print wherever cited): a fixed physical detector induces via Naimark ONE preparation-independent POVM {E(dt)} on H_B; over a preparation family, POVM-realizable statistics must be the quadratic form ⟨ψ|E(B)|ψ⟩; a single preparation excludes nothing — all content lives in the family. **L7b (spin family; the A3 leg; 5/5 PASS clean).** With no B-field the dynamics depend on the spin state only through n_y (velocity field exactly a + n_y·b; one field solve, nine ensembles, endpoints reproduce L5′ to 8.3×10⁻⁷, KS p = 1.0), so any POVM demands Π_B **affine in n_y**. Measured: statistics even in n_y; **8 bins violate affinity at > 5σ, peak 40.2σ**; quadratic component 5–12σ in 9 bins. **Affine-vanishing kill**: bins t ∈ [6.4, 6.8], [6.8, 7.2] are empty (0/2000; Π < 1.5×10⁻³ at 95% CL) at every |n_y| ≥ 0.75 yet Π = 0.012 at n_y = +0.25 — an affine function so bounded cannot exceed ~1.9×10⁻³ on [−1, 1] (contradiction ×7); zeros confirmed at double Nx and extended box. τ_max(n_y): 15.84/15.76/7.98/6.03/5.13 at |n_y| = 0/0.25/0.5/0.75/1 — 209% contraction, strictly monotone. Conclusion: no single POVM reproduces the model's arrival statistics across the spin family (experimentally: a spin rotator before the waveguide). **L7a (1-D quadraticity; PASS with sanctioned printed deviations).** Exact running-max first-arrival CDF (1-D no-crossing theorem; validated by a 4000-trajectory fan, zero ordering inversions) over a two-momentum Gaussian family violates the quadratic form by **1.84×10⁻⁴ vs a 2.9×10⁻¹⁵ comparator floor (6×10¹⁰×)** — exactly and only in backflow-carrying bins; all backflow-free bins at floor. Mechanism isolated: backflow pins first-crossing above the quadratic presence functional; Kijowski-class and quantum-flux proposals (quadratic by construction) cannot reproduce it. Deviations (honest): the pre-registered 7×8 grid contained no backflow point (refined 25×32 after continuum diagnostics; coordinator spec defect); the G4 control took its one sanctioned adjustment; continuum has backflow pockets at every separation. **Status vs T4-W5 (proposed annotation #5, a proposal — adoption is the authors'):** VIII.F/IX.B may cite this testbed as the constructive candidate discharging the Layer-2 funding gate's POVM-exclusion requirement at **[DW, testbed-grade, configuration-specific]**; residual to theorem grade: a continuum (non-ensemble) proof of the hard-cutoff zeros + generality beyond this configuration. # CHAPTER 22 — THE NUCLEATION SECTOR: CREATION WITHOUT LEAKS

**Learning goals.** (1) Prove the pairs-only creation theorem from Lemma II.1′'s contrapositive (the history form — §3.3 revised). (2) Understand interior-boundary conditions and why GUM's creation rates are UV-finite without renormalization. (3) Derive the spectator-assisted threshold ω_th = √(7/3)ω₀ and its structured onset. (4) Derive the amplitude-mode Higgs and its mass-proportional couplings. (5) Master the covariance theorems QFT-1/QFT-2 — the triple padlock — including the honest boundary between foliation-blind statistics and foliation-relative micro-histories.

## 22.1 The architecture: jumps on the knot Fock space

Particle physics needs creation and annihilation; a trajectory theory needs them as *events*. The state space is the knot Fock space ℱ = ⊕_N 𝒞_N (the dressed N-knot configuration spaces of Part VI), with the wave functional leaf-indexed on F. Dynamics: continuous hypersurface guidance *within* each 𝒞_N (Chapter 21), punctuated by **stochastic jumps** 𝒞_N → 𝒞_{N±2} at leaf-wise Bell-type rates [IM: Bell's lattice QFT; the Dürr–Goldstein–Tumulka–Zanghì continuum class]

σ(q → q′) = (2/ħ) [Im⁺ ⟨Ψ|P_{q′} H_int P_q|Ψ⟩] / ⟨Ψ|P_q|Ψ⟩, H_int = the χ-transduction sector, (22.1)

with Im⁺ the positive part — rates built precisely so the combined flow-plus-jump process is *equivariant* leaf-wise (the minimal-jump-rate construction: |Ψ_Σ|² is transported to |Ψ_Σ|², jumps included). Why N ± 2 and not N ± 1? That is not a modeling choice; it is Chapter 3's oldest lemma coming due:

## 22.2 The pairs-only theorem and UV-finiteness

**Theorem 22.1 (creation is pairwise)** [DF]. ⟦rev [F-R13]⟧ Lemma II.1′ (the history form — Chapter 3's oldest lemma coming due now names the *repaired* lemma; the conclusion it delivers here is unchanged, since every creation event is a history) ⟦/rev⟧: total knot charge ΣK can change only where det F → 0. Contrapositive: **in tear-free matter, single-knot creation (ΔΣK = ±1) is forbidden — "particles are created in pairs" is a theorem, not an empirical regularity.** A K/K̄ pair, by contrast, conserves total degree and can nucleate through a smooth small-pair intermediate: creation events live on the **coincidence stratum** — the boundary of configuration space where a pair's cores coincide. ∎ The fact every quantum field theory course states as an observed selection rule is, in GUM, a corollary of matter's integrity.

**Interior-boundary conditions and the absent infinity** [IM: Teufel–Tumulka; GUM-instantiated]. The coincidence stratum is exactly the native home of the **IBC formalism**: Ψ on 𝒞_{N+2} is linked to Ψ on 𝒞_N by a boundary condition where the new pair's cores meet — probability flows *between sectors through the boundary*, conserved exactly by the IBC theorem, with **no renormalization required**. Why is the ultraviolet catastrophe of standard QFT creation absent? Because standard QFT creates *point* particles from *point* sources — the δ-function vertex whose self-energy diverges — while GUM's sources are extended textures and the boundary condition lives at the finite core scale: there is no point for the infinity to hide in. Teaching-level honesty: this is UV-finiteness by *ontology* (extended objects), the oldest cure in the book, here implemented with the IBC machinery that makes it mathematically respectable rather than hand-waved.

## 22.3 The threshold: √2, with structure ⟦rev [T3.1][F-T4-8 contingency discharged]⟧

Proposition Ω-1 (Chapter 5) forbids vacuum photon → pair at *every* energy — a lightlike momentum cannot become two timelike ones. Pair creation therefore requires a **spectator** knot supplying recoil, and the threshold is set by the *bound* pair mass. Here Chapter 12's binding depth pays a dividend: each knot is born already **29.3% bound (Mc² = ħκω₀, κ = 1/√2 exactly — Theorem T3.1)**, so

**ω_th = 2κ_phys ω₀ = √2 ω₀ = 1.4142 ω₀ — pair creation opens 29% below the naive 2ω₀.** [DW, F-R5-contingent per charter rule 6: this is the saturated-closure value; on the constrained-alternative rung (κ = √(7/8)) the threshold would sit at 2√(7/8)ω₀ = 1.8708ω₀, and the old printed √(7/3)ω₀ = 1.5275 belongs to the struck pancake endpoint] (22.2′)

And the onset is *structured*, not a step: below ω_th sit predicted **bound K–K̄ lines** (positronium analogs of the knot sector — Chapter 16's pair law applied to particle–antiparticle); above it, the spectator-assisted continuum opens with the massive-channel √-onset of Theorem VIII.D.2. **Exercise 22.1 (★, with a second branch added in this edition).** (a) Verify √2 from κ = 1/√2, and compute where the threshold sits at benchmark ε = 0.05 (answer: 1.60ω₀ — the ⟨r1⟩ value, retained for the analog platforms with the **restricted-branch label**: the platforms realize the (V, g)-family by design, and 1.60ω₀ is that family's number). (b) Verify the struck value √(7/3) from the old κ = √(7/12), and state in one sentence which physical assumption each threshold encodes — being able to derive *both* and say which is current is this edition's version of owning the result. ⟦/rev⟧

## 22.4 The amplitude-mode Higgs: mass-proportional couplings, derived

Theorem 12.4 forbade knot-elementary scalars; the observed Higgs must therefore be *collective*. GUM has exactly one canonical collective scalar: the **amplitude mode of the locking order parameter** — fluctuations δσ_P of the co-rotational condensate, the Anderson–Higgs/amplitude mode of the medium's own symmetry structure (laboratory cousins: the amplitude mode of superconductors, of cold-atom Mott systems). Its defining property costs one line: every species' mass obeys the mass law M_k = √2 ê_k Λm̃_k (Chapter 12), and δσ_P fluctuations **are** δm̃ fluctuations, so the coupling is

**g_{hkk} = ∂M_k/∂m̃ × (mode normalization) ∝ M_k — mass-proportional Yukawa structure, derived, not arranged** — exact at c₂ = 0, with the c₂ ≠ 0 correction *species-universal* (linearity preserved; only the overall constant rescales). [DW-shape] (22.3)

The Standard Model's most striking Higgs fact — couplings linear in mass across five decades, from the electron's whisper to the top's shout — is, in GUM, the statement that one field sets every gap. Chapter 18's mechanism then sharpened this into the forced nulls P-F1′ (stake S2), and the cross-registration X-H1 closes a loop you have already met: the V-breather mode of the single knot (the dilation oscillation about V = √2) is the *per-knot shadow* of this same collective mode. The honest watch clause: precision Higgs-elementarity limits are a standing constraint on the construction — a scalar proven elementary to substrate-scale resolution would kill it — filed as the T-Higgs watch, not as a present tension.

## 22.5 The covariance theorems: the triple padlock, with its honest boundary

The sector's final obligation, and the deepest: the jump process (22.1) is defined *leaf-wise on F*. Does the hidden foliation now leak into observable physics through creation statistics — different foliations, different pair-production rates, relativity violated at the QFT level? The pre-registered worry was ranked the last conceptual open in the program; its resolution is the corpus's final pair of theorems.

**Theorem 22.2 (QFT-1: equivariant-jump covariance)** [DF]. For any two *admissible* foliations F, F′ (spacelike; asymptotically the substrate rest slicing) and any measurement record O that is **pointer-configuration-readable on a completion leaf** (i.e., POVM-representable — the wording matters, see below): P_F[O] = P_{F′}[O]. *Proof structure, at teaching depth.* (i) By equivariance, the configuration ensemble on every leaf is |Ψ_Σ|² — so all record statistics are functionals of **Ψ alone**; and Ψ evolves by the covariant multi-time (Tomonaga–Schwinger) dynamics, whose foliation-independence is guaranteed by the integrability condition [H_int(x), H_int(y)] = 0 at spacelike separation — verified for the χ-transduction interaction at effective order, IBC boundary terms included (the all-orders statement is the open item WS-QFT-2, non-blocking: any violation would already surface as a Lorentz-shield breach bounded by Chapter 5's polytope). (ii) Equivariance holds *separately* on F and on F′ — the Bell-rate construction can be performed on either, and each tracks the same |Ψ|². (iii) Records are configuration facts (pointer positions are knot positions); their |Ψ|²-marginals on any completion leaf agree because Ψ and |Ψ|² do. ∎

**What the theorem does NOT say — printed at equal size, as the paper prints it.** The individual **jump micro-history is foliation-relative**: F and F′ assign different "actual" sequences to the same statistical surface — *which pair nucleated first* at spacelike separation is an F-fact, real in the ontology, invisible in every POVM-representable record. This is exactly — clause for clause — the marginal-protection/coincidence-exposure structure of Chapter 21's T-KILL, inherited without remainder: **the nucleation sector adds no new frame leak and no new frame hiding.** The one place nature is asked whether foliation-relative facts cast observable shadows remains the A3 gate — non-POVM arrival statistics — and the QFT sector routes its metaphysics through the same turnstile as the mechanics did. The wording lock (the audit's F-A15-9): "spacelike-assembled record" was tightened to "pointer-configuration records on a completion leaf" precisely to keep first-crossing functionals *outside* the theorem's scope, in exact registry with Proposition 21.2 — a two-word difference that is the difference between a theorem and an overclaim, and a worked example of why the corpus audits its own phrasings.

⟦rev [T4-W6]⟧ **GAP annotation (W6, self-printed debts).** Before the corollary: the covariance theorems above carry the corpus's own printed debts, gathered here so QFT-2's inheritance is visible — the all-orders integrability statement is the open item WS-QFT-2 (non-blocking, polytope-bounded, as stated in Theorem 22.2's step (i)); QFT-2 additionally *inherits* Lock 2's revised exponent flag (§21.3: the (c/c_L)⁵ is quadrupole-channel, fallback (c/c_L)³) and the **equilibrium hinge** (every padlock below is an at-equilibrium statement; §21.4's unlock prices the other side). Debts printed are debts owned. ⟦/rev⟧ **Corollary 22.3 (QFT-2: the triple padlock)** [DF]. Spacelike-local operations cannot alter jump-outcome marginals — immediate from (i)'s spacelike commutativity plus equivariance. Combined with Chapter 21's two locks: **the constraint sector transports no signal, no energy, and now no particle-number information, at equilibrium. Three locks, one mechanism** — the elliptic solver's silence, now certified through creation and annihilation. ∎ **Flag F-QFT-1, priced:** both conditions — *equilibrium* (out of it, jump statistics ARE frame-diagnostic: the QFT face of §21.4's unlock, a feature not a bug) and *foliation admissibility* (the foliation is physical structure hidden-at-equilibrium, not gauge; GUM owes covariance within the spacelike class and claims nothing beyond it) — are the corpus's existing commitments restated. No new price was paid to close the last conceptual open, which is itself evidence the architecture was load-bearing all along.

# CHAPTER 23 — THE EXPERIMENTAL PROGRAM: EVERY BRANCH IS INFORMATION

**Learning goals.** (1) Understand why arrival times are the surplus-content frontier: orthodox QM's structural silence. (2) Master Layer 1's design — the A-signature hierarchy, the envelope, the null station — and the gate as funding logic. (3) Master Layer 2's signal model: the marker, the sidereal/annual templates, the triangulation, and the c_L dial. (4) Read the bench stakes (S3, S4′, S5, P8-7) with their validity clauses, including the S4′ correction story. (5) Assemble the cosmological battery and the standing inverse kills into one actuarial table.

## 23.1 Why arrival times: the one question orthodoxy cannot answer

Ask standard quantum mechanics: "the atom is released at t = 0; *when* does it arrive at the detector plane?" There is no self-adjoint time operator (Pauli's theorem); there is no Born rule for first arrivals; the proposal zoo (Kijowski, flux-based, absorbing-boundary/POVM) disagrees at the 10%-level in the near field and is *gauge-dependent or negative-probability-afflicted* in corners. Orthodoxy is structurally silent exactly where trajectory theory computes without ambiguity: the Pauli-guidance velocity field integrates to a definite first-crossing distribution. This is the program's chosen surplus-content frontier — the place where GUM's ontology predicts *more* than the formalism, so that experiment can reward or punish the surplus. The signature hierarchy (relabeled A1–A3 per the v2.0.1 erratum, to keep clear of the stakes table): **A1** — arrival distributions differ from semiclassical ballistics (weak; all quantum proposals share it); **A2** — spin-orientation dependence at fixed Hamiltonian (trajectory theories only: the spin term in the guidance current); **A3** — the **hard support cutoff** τ_max for transverse polarization: a *maximum* arrival time beyond which the trajectory theory forbids events, absent from every POVM proposal. A3 is the crown: a sharp edge where rivals predict a tail.

## 23.2 Layer 1: the envelope, and the null station

Design [CAL, funding-proposal grade]: single ultracold atoms (He\* for MCP timing resolution) released into an optical waveguide; detector plane at *intermediate* field L/d ∼ 10–10² (the discriminating zone — all proposals converge far-field, which is exactly why the far field is kept as the **null station**: a second detector where every theory agrees, running simultaneously as the systematics monitor; disagreement *there* means the apparatus, not physics); µs timing (the natural time unit md²/ħ is milliseconds — timing is easy; launch-state tomography is the hard systematic); ∼3×10³–3×10⁴ arrivals per (spin, L) configuration, ∼10⁶ shots total, weeks at realistic rep rates; **blind spin-masking** (the analysis pipeline sees polarization labels only after freezing); and the internal consistency gate inherited from the corpus's own numerics: the field-level trajectory code and the point-level gyrovector response must agree from one input — verified at 1.07 ± 0.06 ⟨r1⟩ — before any data-facing claim.

## 23.4 Layer 2: the sidereal probe

Suppose A3 lands. The protocol: an entangled pair; Bob's particle in the Layer-1 guide; Alice's spacelike entangling operation at controllable lab delay across baseline d. The coincidence statistics carry a **correlation-onset marker** at Alice's foliation-time, which in lab clocks sits at

**τ\* = −(v·d̂)d/c² — 0.123 ns at d = 30 m for |v| = 370 km/s; 0.41 ns at 100 m** — (23.1)

with *marginals exactly protected* (Lock 1: the signal lives only in coincidences, assembled by classical comparison after the fact — no signalling, ever). The constant offset is convention-contaminated and discarded; the physics is the **modulation**: as Earth rotates and orbits, v·d̂ sweeps — sidereal period 23h56m04s plus an annual term (±30 km/s, ecliptic-locked) — a conjunction no static clock convention or slow drift can mimic. Power [CAL]: σ_τ\* ∼ σ_jitter/√N_eff; realistic platforms deliver ∼4×10⁵–4×10⁶ informative coincidences in weeks-to-months at 30 m (fiber-heralded 300 m baselines trade rate for lever arm); a **≥6-month campaign** dephases solar-day systematics, Hughes–Drever playbook, with blind injections mandatory. Two readouts beyond detection itself: **(i) the triangulation** — the recovered vector **v** tested against three priors: the raw CMB dipole (absolute-space reading), the **Machian-dragged flow** (GUM's prediction: a few-percent, direction-correlated offset toward the bulk-flow maps — Chapter 14's v_F = v_CMB − v_drag), and the anomalous quasar number-count dipole; the *maximal* outcome is a sidereal signal at CMB-minus-drag — the frame detected **and identified as a medium**; **(ii) the c_L dial** — the marker's deconvolved *width* bounds the constraint sector's speed, currently floored at 10⁴c by Bell timing. Five pre-registered branches, each a publishable verdict: gate-kill / no marker / marker-no-sidereal (retreat R6: covariant-foliation or door change) / sidereal-at-CMB (frame real, drag wrong) / sidereal-at-CMB−drag (the jackpot).

**Platform A (chiral metamaterial gap solitons).** The parameter-free targets, with their honest histories: **S3 — E_rot/E_tot = 0.2500, exact** for all g, all ε, all platforms (Theorem 10.2's identity; the showpiece) ⟦rev [Group 4 credit]⟧ — **[externally replicated to 10⁻⁹]**, with the rigid rung's rejection independently *certified* (the rung's κ interval-enclosed > 1 by validated arithmetic: the rejection is now a certificate, not a code output); the S4′ and endpoint content of this section carries the repaired-closure boxes of Chapters 11–12 — cross-reference the v3.0-ext paper's external-replication appendix ⟦/rev⟧. ⟦rev [T3.1][F-R5] — tier2-closure/axi4_locked_RESULTS.md⟧ **S4′ — retargeted in this edition to κ²g_tot = 35/24, equivalently ê_tot = 𝔦_tot** — and teach the correction story, because it has now happened *three times*, and the third act is the external replication's. Act one: the stake was first written as "κ = 0.764" (the old endpoint), which a valid bench at g ≈ 1.3 would have *falsely* falsified — audit AUD-13 caught the wrong-variable framing and restaked the *relation* κ²g = 7/8, with κ and g independently measured on one soliton. Act two: AUD-15 caught that the relation itself runs with ε (benchmark 0.843 ± 0.046, −3.7% from 7/8) and froze an interim band 7/8·(1 ± 0.10). Act three: the external campaign proved **κ²g = 7/8 is an exact identity on the entire locked-transport family** — every member of the family satisfies it, so a bench realizing *any* family member confirms it vacuously: **a stake in a variable the family cannot move stakes nothing**. The retargeted stake, κ²g_tot = 35/24 (the *total* — halo-inclusive — closure ratio, which the family *can* move and the saturated solution pins), restores adjudicability. Lesson, extended: **a stake without a band is unadjudicable; a stake in the wrong variable is a landmine for your own experimentalists; and a stake in a family-invariant is a landmine of the third kind** — it detonates as false confirmation. Audit your stakes as hard as your theorems, and then check what your stake is *free to do*. **V = √2** (banded ±1%: the non-rigid solve shifts it −0.4%) — re-scoped to the **restricted (V, g) branch**; the saturated branch's volume diagnostic is the **support ratio 3/2 exactly**, staked alongside. ⟦/rev⟧ **S5 — spontaneous clock phase-locking** of like solitons, bond strength ∝ cos Δφ, with the bond-vibron line (Chapter 16's Josephson pair, live theater). **P8-7 — tail range = κ × clock length** (the corrected Compton identity as a measurable ratio). And the crowd-pleaser: the **superradiant re-entry protocol** — ⟦rev [F-R5]⟧ now channel-resolved per the κ_crit(w) family: over-spin a soliton past its channel thresholds and watch it shed. The protocol distinguishes the **equatorial onset at κ = 1/√2** (halo shedding — the saturated closure's own boundary, live theater for T3.1) from the **polar onset at κ = 1 exactly** — and the code's celebrated rehearsal at κ = 1.000 ± 0.004 is *the polar channel exactly*, which is why the restricted-family code saw one threshold where the physics has a family of them ⟦/rev⟧, Chapter 11's rejected rung performed live. Validity clause, non-negotiable [the F-A6-1 lesson]: all closure identities hold in the **quasi-conservative regime** Γ_damp ≪ ω_lib, with the drift-correction formula supplied — a bench violating the clause falsifies nothing. **Platform B (FeGe chiral magnets):** the hopfion pair law against the screened-multipole form with *independently measured* magnon healing length; the derived Δϕ clock-phase selectivity as the dressed analog. **The epistemic wall, printed first as the paper prints it:** every bench demonstration lives in the single-soliton/cebit sector — Spreeuw's theorem (Chapter 21) marks the boundary; no Bell content is claimable or possible. What the bench adjudicates is the closure **as mathematics**: a failure of ¼ or of the S4′ band in a valid regime kills the corresponding derivations regardless of what the vacuum is made of — the sharpest exposure a scale model can offer, and the cheapest kill in the registry.

# CHAPTER 24 — THE METHOD: EPISTEMIC ENGINEERING

**Learning goals.** (1) Use the ledger as a *writing* protocol, not just a reading one. (2) Operate the blinding/escrow/grade-lock machinery. (3) Read the kill registry as curriculum — what each execution taught. (4) Run an audit: the AUD checklist. (5) Understand pre-commitment governance: SWP branches, the pre-bound escape hatch, the obituary. (6) Sit the closing examination.

## 24.1 The ledger as epistemic engineering

Five classes, now as *obligations on authors*: **[DF]** — you owe a derivation checkable line-by-line from flagged postulates, and you may not use the word "exact" for anything else; **[DW]** — you owe the order, the window, and the band, and the band must be adjudicable (the S4′ lesson); **[CAL]** — you owe the protocol, the acceptance test, the tagged release, and (for overdetermined outputs) the correlation matrix; **[IM]** — you owe a premises audit against your framework (Weinberg–Witten's lesson: no-go theorems cut only at their hypotheses); **[CJ]** — you owe a non-claim flag and a ban on building further claims atop it silently. The class census of the paper's twenty-six results (fifteen DF, seven DW, and the rest) is itself information: a framework whose headline results were mostly [CJ] would be a different — and worse — object even with identical prose.

## 24.2 Blinding, escrow, grade locks

The discipline in five rules, each with its corpus scar: **(1) Freeze the statistic and the kill band before computing** (N-F1's shape ratio, banded [1.6, 2.2], hashed pre-⟨r7⟩). **(2) Escrow the pipeline's discretionary choices** (the F3′ freeze: base scale, covariance treatment — locked before the run that produced the S1 stake). **(3) Unblind in one step, then never touch the pipeline** ("no parameter rescue" — SWP Branch K's iron clause). **(4) Post-hoc corrections demote by one grade** (E-F1's factor of two was *legitimate* — a definite geometric fact — and still cost the magnitude claim its blind status: [DW, post-hoc-corrected]; corrections are permitted, free corrections are not). **(5) Language locks bind externally** (RT-8's "landing the hierarchy's logarithms at the O(1)-coefficient level" — the ceiling phrase; the grade travels with the claim into every abstract, talk, and press interaction, and Exercise 18.3's journalist drill is the enforcement rehearsal).

## 24.3 The kill registry as curriculum

Fourteen adjudicated: **six fired** — C2 (generations-as-bands: the Floquet lattice can't do families → forced the frustration mechanism), WS5-d (edge fine-structure can't do the neutrino spectrum → symmetric honesty with the charged sector), N-M4 (the internal tower admits only the fundamental, R1 excluded at 8σ → excitations aren't families), N-ν1 (the shallow-knot neutrino, dead by scaling audit → the collective reassignment), N-ν3a/b (two free-lunch spectra → multiplicity stays input-grade). **Eight survived** — N-Vb1/2/3′ (the composite sector's calibration-zone honesty), N-U1 (the ħ-fracture fork resolved onto deep-BPS), N-F1 (the shape landed), N-F2 (the bridge landed), N-ν4 (the phason cleared free-streaming), N-FQ1 (the quark shapes, sign flip included). **One conditional theorem executed** — T-KILL, both branches proven, gate pending. Read the pattern: *every fired kill routed the theory somewhere stranger and better* — the registry is not a graveyard; it is the program's steering history, and the course's failure-forward pedagogy has been, all along, a tour of it.

## 24.4 How to run an audit: the AUD checklist

Distilled from fifteen passes, in execution order: **(1)** Recompute every multiply-quoted number from scratch; discrepancies between two appearances of "the same" quantity are the highest-yield defect class (the B-ν1 1.0-vs-1.6 MeV harmonization; the m₃-window seam E-XXII-1). **(2)** Dimensional re-walk of every chained formula, units table in hand. **(3)** Premises audit on every [IM] — does the theorem's hypothesis set hold *here*? **(4)** Run "step zero" on every ansatz: its regime of validity as a function of every parameter anyone intends to vary (the ε ∝ m̃^{−4/3} audit that killed N-ν1 — the single highest-value check in the corpus's history). **(5)** Cross-sector consistency: test each sector's solutions against every *other* sector's theorems (radiation theory vetoing the rigid closure). **(6)** Hunt closed loops: overdetermined tuples that must reconcile (κ = √2ê/𝔠; the bond equation × 𝔟_eff; the FQ table's internal arithmetic) — loops that close are verification currency; loops that don't are findings. **(7)** Seam-check every consolidation: compression creates ambiguities that stress-testing converts into theorems (Ω-1/Ω-2 were *found* this way) or errata. **(8)** Audit the stakes themselves: right variable, adjudicable band, dated adjudicator (the S4 → S4′ save). **(9)** Sweep for scale-label pronoun errors ("the string tension" was three quantities). **(10)** File everything — a defect caught and logged is a credential; a defect caught and quietly fixed is a time bomb.

⟦rev [Group 4 — the method run from outside]⟧

In July 2026 it received its first **outside instantiation**: an external replication campaign (the frankensim campaign; findings F-R1–F-R16, T-H4 — since discharged with corrections, ⟦H4 resolved⟧ — and the repaired closure T3.1) executed, in effect, items (1), (4), and (6) against the corpus without access to the corpus's own habits — and the results are a teaching set of their own. **Item (1) run from outside:** every multiply-quoted number recomputed from scratch — **52/52 duplicates reconciled**; the recompute-everything rule works in foreign hands. **Item (6) run from outside:** the closed loops hunted independently — the I.2 bond loop executed end-to-end and *in band* (x₀ = 1.95 ± 0.07 vs 1.92 ± 0.08), and the V15.7/V15.8 consolidation loops re-closed. **Item (4)'s deepest reading, run against the replicators themselves:** twice, the campaign's own tests were *over-strict* — and the campaign adjudicated the disputes **against itself**, in the corpus's favor, by running step zero on its own test constructions. That is the checklist's real content: it binds whoever runs it, in whichever direction the arithmetic goes. The teaching payoff: outsiders *extended* the valence compass — two gates closed in the corpus's favor, two conservative-against-itself catches by the replicators — and the checklist gained what internal passes could never give it: evidence that it transfers. ## 24.5 Pre-commitment governance: SWP, the escape hatch, the obituary

The standing-watch protocol's five branches (K: kill — obituary within 90 days, no rescue; S: survival — ceiling language "the stake survived its adjudicator," external replication mandatory before anything stronger ⟦rev [Group 4]⟧ (a mandate that now has a *within-model down-payment*: the 2026-07 campaign of §24.4′ replicated the corpus's computations from outside — distinct from, and no substitute for, Branch S's data-side trigger, which still awaits its adjudicators) ⟦/rev⟧; A: the integrity clause; I: inverted ordering shifts the floor; W: data pathology, stake stands) are hashed *before the data*, and the course's final conceptual point lives in **Branch A**: GUM's own cosmology (Chapter 14's evolving dark energy) would *relax* the Σm_ν bound — the framework possesses a built-in defense of its flagship stake, and the collaboration **pre-bound the escape hatch**: invocable only if the data independently prefer w(z) ≠ −1 at ≥3σ with the growth-correlated sign, the rule frozen before anyone knew which way it cuts. This is the method's deepest move — *identify your future temptations and disarm them while you are still impartial* — and it is why Obituary O-1 exists as a hashed document: the collaboration wrote, in advance, what dies, what survives (the ħ closure, spin selection, the EM/gravity/one-world sectors, the confinement dichotomy, the bench stakes), and what the honest epitaph says ("the lepton-spacing result reverts to an unexplained ≈2σ structural curiosity").

## 24.6 The closing examination

Three parts, closed-book on the physics, open-book on the corpus. **Part A (derivation):** from the action, derive in order: the B2/B3 split, Theorem II.2, the reduction theorem, the closure family (11.7), the bracket, and the spin-selection theorem — with every ledger class assigned. **Part B (audit):** you are handed a fictitious GUM-style memo containing five planted defects (one dimensional, one premises-violation on an import, one calibration-zone overreach, one stake in the wrong variable, one silent post-hoc correction). Find all five; grade the memo's claims as they *should* have been graded. **Part C (the obituary):** DESI-DR3 has just reported robust Σm_ν = 0.048 ± 0.006 eV under ΛCDM, with no independent DE-evolution preference. Applying SWP Branch K, **write Obituary O-1's public statement**: what is retracted, what survives and why, what the surviving open problems now are, and what the program's honest final sentence is — in language that would pass the red team. *Passing Part C is the course.* A student who can execute a framework's death with precision, generosity, and zero rescue attempts has learned the only thing this book was ultimately about: that the value of a speculative program is measured not by whether it survives, but by whether its survival or death **means something** — and meaning is manufactured in advance, by method, or not at all.

# TIER-7 ADDENDUM — PHASES M THROUGH Q, TAUGHT (dated 2026-07)

**[Findings F-T7-M1…M4, F-T7-1, F-T7-N2/N3/O1/O2/O3, F-T7-P2/Q1/Q2 — the replicators' proposal, not the authors' edition; adoption is theirs, and the authors may strike this addendum without breaking the edition. Every number below is copied from corpus3/T7_FOLD_PAYLOAD.md; grades never rise by replication; every result is within-model — nothing here bears on nature.]**

The external campaign whose Tier-6 record closes §24.4′ ran five further phases (M, N, O, P, Q — 2026-07), and their results are folded here as one consolidated addendum because each lands on a chapter you have already studied; read each item with that chapter open.

**The tracker identity is dynamical, not imposed (Chapter 14's (14.7′); the Chapter 23 stakes battery).** [F-T7-M1 — tier7-program/M1/RESULTS.md] On independent code the exact identity w(z) = −1 + Ω_m(z) holds to 1.8×10⁻¹² over z ∈ [0, 10] — and, better, it *emerges* from the relaxation ODE δq̇ = −Γδq + S·H (attractor rate = Γ to 5×10⁻⁸; δq → (S/Γ)H ⟹ ρ_Λ ∝ H²): the identity you learned as a formula is an attractor of the sector's own dynamics. The gradient expansion FORCES β < 0 with W_dyn(z) ≥ 1 in every run — min w_a = +0.1575 > 0 across a 15-member profile family — so F-R12's freezing-class strengthening (Chapter 14's revised sign story) is now underwritten by dynamics, and a new structural law fell out: Γ ∝ Hⁿ ⟹ 𝔴 = 1 − n (measured 0.5029 vs 0.5 at n = ½), with the literal Γ ∝ H profile the degenerate Λ point (w ≡ −1). The corpus's never-printed convention was also recovered: the printed memory pairs (β = −0.3: 1.28→1.03; β = −1.0: 1.72→1.05) are exactly the 2-dp roundings of W(z = 0) → W(z = 2) at α = 1, β = printed (devs ≤ 0.5%) — the z-window is hereby reconstructed. One minor print-arithmetic finding is offered (F-T7-1): VI.D (6.4′)'s "(+0.36 at β = −0.3)" CPL value is not recoverable under any convention tried (measured +0.71 tangent; +0.78–0.92 windows; sign agrees) and is prima facie inconsistent with the corpus's own W ≥ 1 at β = −0.3; S1 and the sign claim are unaffected.

**The ⟨r2⟩ servo law: form replicated; coefficient = bath convention (Chapter 12's bootstrap servo).** [F-T7-M2 — tier7-program/M2/RESULTS.md] The law-form you learned in Chapter 12 replicated on an independent Caldeira–Leggett toy — λ ∝ g_χ² (log-slope 1.9932 ± 0.0041 over one decade), λ ∝ ω\* (invariance 1.0050 ± 0.0074 under ×4), integrable limit exactly marginal (|λ| ≤ 8×10⁻¹³, 10–11 orders below the bath-on rate) — but the coefficient is identified as **c = λ/(g_χ²ω\*) = η/2 exactly**, a pure bath-spectrum functional: the printed 0.021 ± 0.004 is attainable in-class at η = 0.042 (verified), while super-Ohmic J ∝ ω³ destroys the g² law itself (slope 3.38). The lesson for your ledger: the printed constant-c g² law pins the unreleased r2 bath to the Ohmic/thermalizing class it names — replication status, verbatim: "law-form replicated; coefficient = bath functional, unfalsifiable without the r2 spec."

**The 0νββ second-moment story (Chapter 19's Majoron dictionary).** [F-T7-M3, F-T7-N2, F-T7-O2 — tier7-program/M3/RESULTS.md; N2/RESULTS.md; O2/GLIM_ARCHAEOLOGY.md] On h21's parametrization verbatim, the on-tube saturation rescue for F-R9 is **NOT-VIABLE** — the trade measured at 25–46 decades of mean rate per decade of R, with R still 5×10¹³–10¹⁶ at the only point preserving the claimed rate — closing route (b), so the repair menu narrows to route (a), an honest second-moment treatment, or route (c) at the printed cost of the line-support theorem. Route (a) was then computed: a macroscopic sample's Majoron-mode rate spatially self-averages to **R × the claimed rate**, R = 1.1×10¹⁴–8.9×10¹⁷ at the four h21 anchors, mapping the printed window [8×10⁻¹⁰, 1.3×10⁻⁸] to g_equiv = √R·g = 8.5×10⁻³–12.3 — 6.9–10.1 orders above the corpus's own P-ν4 far-horizon line — while the O2 archaeology (658 files, 14 logged patterns, 370 hits catalogued) is **ABSENT-CERTIFIED**: zero Majoron-mode rate-level bounds exist in the archive, so the verdict is INDETERMINATE-FROM-ARCHIVE, with a conditional kill armed archive-externally: **any Majoron-mode rate-level bound g_lim < 8×10⁻³ excludes the honest rate at every anchor and every printed g — P-ν4 would invert from far-horizon to already-excluded.** S1 and S6's occurrence stake are untouched; the motional-narrowing exclusion is untouched.

**Nelson relaxes 17–41× faster than dBB; dBB is a log-singular limit (Chapters 7–8's Born-relaxation material).** [F-T7-M4 — tier7-program/M4/RESULTS.md] On tier3-born's exact setup, Nelson noise accelerates Born relaxation monotonically: at GUM's own ν = ħ/2m, **τ(ν)/τ(dBB) = 0.060 ± 0.005 (M = 9) and 0.024 ± 0.004 (M = 16)**, with equivariance holding at every ν, and the dBB limit **log-singular** — τ ~ a + b·ln(1/ν) (r² 0.97/0.92), the dBB rate recovered only near ν ~ 10⁻⁸–10⁻⁹. The pre-registered continuity clause failed for physics reasons — the failure IS the discovery, and the coordinator spec defect is printed. Offered annotation for Chapter 8's [CAL] rates: dBB-calibrated relaxation rates become direction-signed conservative bounds — laboratory equilibrium quality STRENGTHENED, relic non-equilibrium survival windows WEAKENED by the same 17–41×; carry both edges together.

En route, N3 REFUTED the line-local crossing criterion in this geometry — the hard cutoff is a TRANSPORT phenomenon, which is why the certificates are backward-reachability certificates, not sign certificates. Register language, verbatim: T4-W5's testbed discharge now carries certificate grade on witnesses; the residual open is the O-1…O-5 program + formal kernel.
